1	---

11	
12	# QrCode
13	
14	A declarative component that takes an input string and renders a QR code matrix.
15	There is no user interaction state machine — `QrCode` is stateless.
16	
17	Supporting types used by Props and the API:
18	
19	```rust
20	/// Error correction level for QR encoding.
21	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
22	pub enum QrErrorCorrection {
23	    /// ~7% recovery.
24	    Low,
25	    /// ~15% recovery.
26	    #[default]
27	    Medium,
28	    /// ~25% recovery.
29	    Quartile,
30	    /// ~30% recovery.
31	    High,
32	}
33	
34	/// The QR code matrix — a 2D grid of modules (black/white cells).
35	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
36	pub struct QrMatrix {
37	    /// Row-major module data. `true` = dark module.
38	    pub modules: Vec<Vec<bool>>,
39	    /// Size (number of modules per side).
40	    pub size: usize,
41	}
42	
43	impl QrMatrix {
44	    /// Create a QrMatrix from a pre-computed module grid.
45	    ///
46	    /// QR matrix generation is delegated to the `qrcode` crate in the adapter
47	    /// layer (`ars-dom`). The core spec defines the rendering contract only.
48	    pub fn new(modules: Vec<Vec<bool>>) -> Self {
49	        let size = modules.len();
50	        Self { modules, size }
51	    }
52	
53	    /// Get the module value at (row, col).
54	    pub fn get(&self, row: usize, col: usize) -> bool {
55	        debug_assert!(row < self.size && col < self.size, "QrMatrix::get() out of bounds: ({row}, {col}) for size {}", self.size);
56	        self.modules.get(row).and_then(|r| r.get(col)).copied().unwrap_or(false)
57	    }
58	}
59	```
60	
61	## 1. API
62	
63	### 1.1 Props
64	
65	```rust
66	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]
67	pub struct Props {
68	    /// Component instance ID.
69	    pub id: String,
70	    /// The data to encode.
71	    pub value: String,
72	    /// Error correction level.
73	    pub error_correction: QrErrorCorrection,
74	    /// Size of each module in pixels (for rendering).
75	    pub module_size: f64,
76	    /// Quiet zone (border) in modules. Standard is 4.
77	    pub quiet_zone: usize,
78	    /// Foreground color (dark modules).
79	    pub foreground: String,
80	    /// Background color (light modules).
81	    pub background: String,
82	    /// Optional image overlay (e.g., logo) in the center.
83	    pub overlay_src: Option<String>,
84	    /// Overlay size as a fraction of the QR code size [0.0, 0.5].
85	    pub overlay_size: f64,
86	}
87	
88	impl Default for Props {
89	    fn default() -> Self {
90	        Self {
91	            id: String::new(),
92	            value: String::new(),
93	            error_correction: QrErrorCorrection::Medium,
94	            module_size: 4.0,
95	            quiet_zone: 4,
96	            foreground: "#000000".into(),
97	            background: "#ffffff".into(),
98	            overlay_src: None,
99	            overlay_size: 0.2,
100	        }
101	    }
102	}
103	```
104	
105	### 1.2 Connect / API
106	
107	```rust
108	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
109	#[scope = "qr-code"]
110	pub enum Part {
111	    Root,
112	    Frame,
113	    Pattern,
114	    Overlay,
115	    DownloadTrigger,
116	}
117	
118	pub struct Api<'a> {
119	    props: &'a Props,
120	    matrix: Option<QrMatrix>,
121	    id: String,
122	    locale: Locale,
123	    messages: Messages,
124	}
125	
126	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
127	    pub fn new(props: &'a Props, env: &Env, messages: &Messages) -> Self {
128	        let matrix = QrMatrix::generate(&props.value, props.error_correction);
129	        let id = if props.id.is_empty() { generate_id() } else { props.id.clone() };
130	        let locale = env.locale.clone();
131	        let messages = messages.clone();
132	        Self { props, matrix, id, locale, messages }
133	    }
134	
135	    pub fn matrix(&self) -> Option<&QrMatrix> {
136	        self.matrix.as_ref()
137	    }
138	
139	    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including quiet zone).
140	    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {
141	        if let Some(ref m) = self.matrix {
142	            (m.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.props.module_size
143	        } else {
144	            0.0
145	        }
146	    }
147	
148	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
149	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
150	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
151	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
152	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
153	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, &self.id);
154	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "img");
155	        let label = if self.props.value.starts_with("http://") || self.props.value.starts_with("https://") {
156	            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)
157	        } else {
158	            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)
159	        };
160	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), label);
161	        let size = self.pixel_size();
162	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!("{}px", size));
163	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!("{}px", size));
164	        attrs
165	    }
166	
167	    pub fn frame_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
168	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
169	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Frame.data_attrs();
170	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
171	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
172	        attrs
173	    }
174	
175	    pub fn pattern_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
176	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
177	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Pattern.data_attrs();
178	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
179	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
180	        attrs
181	    }
182	
183	    pub fn overlay_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
184	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
185	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Overlay.data_attrs();
186	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
187	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
188	        if let Some(ref src) = self.props.overlay_src {
189	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Src, src);
190	        }
191	        attrs
192	    }
193	
194	    pub fn download_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
195	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
196	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::DownloadTrigger.data_attrs();
197	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
198	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
199	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button");
200	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.messages.download_label)(&self.locale));
201	        attrs
202	    }
203	}
204	
205	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
206	    type Part = Part;
207	
208	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
209	        match part {
210	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
211	            Part::Frame => self.frame_attrs(),
212	            Part::Pattern => self.pattern_attrs(),
213	            Part::Overlay => self.overlay_attrs(),
214	            Part::DownloadTrigger => self.download_trigger_attrs(),
215	        }
216	    }
217	}
218	```
219	
220	## 2. Anatomy
221	
222	```text
223	QrCode
224	├── Root             (required — role="img", container for the QR code)
225	├── Frame            (optional — decorative frame around the code)
226	├── Pattern          (required — the actual QR module grid, rendered as SVG or Canvas)
227	├── Overlay          (optional — centered image/logo)
228	└── DownloadTrigger  (optional — button to download QR code as image)
229	```
230	
231	| Part            | Element    | Key Attributes                          |
232	| --------------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------- |
233	| Root            | `<div>`    | `role="img"`, `aria-label`, sized to QR |
234	| Frame           | `<div>`    | decorative border                       |
235	| Pattern         | `<svg>`    | QR module grid rendering                |
236	| Overlay         | `<img>`    | `src` from `overlay_src` prop           |
237	| DownloadTrigger | `<button>` | `aria-label` from messages              |
238	
239	## 3. Accessibility
240	
241	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
242	
243	| Part | Role  | Properties                                                     |
244	| ---- | ----- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
245	| Root | `img` | `aria-label` — `"QR code: {value}"` (or link variant for URLs) |
246	
247	The QR code is a purely visual element. The `aria-label` conveys the encoded data.
248	If the data is a URL, the label indicates that (e.g., `"QR code linking to {url}"`).
249	
250	## 4. Internationalization
251	
252	### 4.1 Messages
253	
254	```rust
255	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
256	pub struct Messages {
257	    /// Root aria-label template (default: "QR code: {value}").
258	    pub label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
259	    /// Label when value is a URL (default: "QR code linking to {url}").
260	    pub link_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
261	    /// Label for the download trigger button (default: "Download QR code").
262	    pub download_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
263	}
264	
265	impl Default for Messages {
266	    fn default() -> Self {
267	        Self {
268	            label: MessageFn::new(|value, _locale| format!("QR code: {value}")),
269	            link_label: MessageFn::new(|url, _locale| format!("QR code linking to {url}")),
270	            download_label: MessageFn::static_str("Download QR code"),
271	        }
272	    }
273	}
274	
275	impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
276	```
277	
278	| Key                      | Default (en-US)              | Purpose                   |
279	| ------------------------ | ---------------------------- | ------------------------- |
280	| `qr_code.label`          | `"QR code: {value}"`         | Root aria-label template  |
281	| `qr_code.link_label`     | `"QR code linking to {url}"` | Label when value is a URL |
282	| `qr_code.download_label` | `"Download QR code"`         | Download trigger label    |
283	
284	The `root_attrs()` method formats `aria-label` from `messages.label` (or `messages.link_label` when the value is a URL), replacing `{value}` / `{url}` with the encoded data.
285	
286	- **RTL**: QR codes are direction-agnostic (fixed matrix). No RTL adjustments needed for the pattern itself. Surrounding layout elements obey the document direction.
287	
288	## 5. Library Parity
289	
290	> Compared against: Ark UI (`QrCode`).
291	
292	### 5.1 Props
293	
294	| Feature                     | ars-ui                      | Ark UI                   | Notes                                   |
295	| --------------------------- | --------------------------- | ------------------------ | --------------------------------------- |
296	| `value` / `defaultValue`    | `value`                     | `value` / `defaultValue` | ars-ui is always controlled (stateless) |
297	| `errorCorrection`           | `error_correction`          | `encoding`               | Equivalent (different grouping)         |
298	| `moduleSize`                | `module_size`               | `pixelSize`              | Equivalent (different naming)           |
299	| `foreground` / `background` | `foreground` / `background` | --                       | ars-ui has color props                  |
300	| `overlaySrc`                | `overlay_src`               | --                       | ars-ui has overlay support              |
301	| `quietZone`                 | `quiet_zone`                | --                       | ars-ui has quiet zone                   |
302	
303	**Gaps:** None.
304	
305	### 5.2 Anatomy
306	
307	| Part            | ars-ui            | Ark UI            | Notes      |
308	| --------------- | ----------------- | ----------------- | ---------- |
309	| Root            | `Root`            | `Root`            | Equivalent |
310	| Frame           | `Frame`           | `Frame`           | Equivalent |
311	| Pattern         | `Pattern`         | `Pattern`         | Equivalent |
312	| Overlay         | `Overlay`         | `Overlay`         | Equivalent |
313	| DownloadTrigger | `DownloadTrigger` | `DownloadTrigger` | Equivalent |
314	
315	**Gaps:** None.
316	
317	### 5.3 Events
318	
319	| Callback     | ars-ui                 | Ark UI          | Notes                                |
320	| ------------ | ---------------------- | --------------- | ------------------------------------ |
321	| Value change | (stateless, re-render) | `onValueChange` | ars-ui is stateless; value is a prop |
322	
323	**Gaps:** None.
324	
325	### 5.4 Features
326	
327	| Feature                 | ars-ui                  | Ark UI |
328	| ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ------ |
329	| QR matrix generation    | Yes (via adapter crate) | Yes    |
330	| Error correction levels | Yes (4 levels)          | Yes    |
331	| Center overlay/logo     | Yes                     | Yes    |
332	| Download as image       | Yes (`DownloadTrigger`) | Yes    |
333	| Custom colors           | Yes                     | No     |
334	| Quiet zone control      | Yes                     | No     |
335	
336	**Gaps:** None.
337	
338	### 5.5 Summary
339	
340	- **Overall:** Full parity.
341	- **Divergences:** ars-ui is fully stateless (QR code is a pure function of `value`). Ark UI supports controlled/uncontrolled value.
342	- **Recommended additions:** None.
343

        (Data("ars-variant"), String("subtle")),
    ],
    styles: [],
}
```

The `attrs` array contains tuples of `(HtmlAttr, AttrValue)` — notice `data-ars-scope` and `data-ars-part` are always first two entries (generated by `Part::data_attrs()`).

### 4. **Spec-Conformance Tests**

**Location:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/`

**Helper function** (helper.rs lines 24-75):
```rust
pub(crate) fn assert_anatomy<P>(expected_scope: &'static str, expected_parts: &[(P, &'static str)])
where
    P: ComponentPart + core::fmt::Debug + PartialEq,
{
    assert_eq!(P::scope(), expected_scope);
    
    let actual = P::all();
    let actual_names = actual.iter().map(ComponentPart::name).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let expected_names = expected_parts.iter().map(|(_, name)| *name).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    
    assert_eq!(actual_names, expected_names);
    // ... validates each part round-trips through `name()` ...
    assert_eq!(&P::ROOT, first_expected);
}
```

**Example spec test** (data_display.rs lines 77-89):
```rust
#[test]
fn meter_anatomy_matches_spec() {
    assert_anatomy(
        "meter",
        &[
            (meter::Part::Root, "root"),
            (meter::Part::Label, "label"),
            (meter::Part::Track, "track"),
            (meter::Part::Range, "range"),
            (meter::Part::ValueText, "value-text"),
        ],
    );
}
```

**What it validates:**
1. `Part::scope()` matches `"meter"` → `data-ars-scope="meter"`
2. `Part::all()` produces exactly the declared parts in order
3. Each part's `name()` returns the kebab-case string
4. `Part::ROOT` (first variant) equals `meter::Part::Root`

### 5. **`ComponentPart` Derive Macro + `data_attrs()`**

**Derive macro source:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/crates/ars-derive/src/component_part.rs`

**Macro expansion** (lines 104-156 of component_part.rs):
```rust
impl ::ars_core::ComponentPart for Part {
    const ROOT: Self = Self::Root;

    fn scope() -> &'static str {
        "badge"  // from #[scope = "badge"]
    }

    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Root => "root",  // generated via heck::ToKebabCase
        }
    }

    fn all() -> ::ars_core::__private::Vec<Self> {
        ::ars_core::__private::Vec::from([Self::Root])
    }
}
```

**`data_attrs()` method** (NOT in macro, defined in trait, from ars-core/src/lib.rs lines 629-635):
```rust
pub trait ComponentPart: Clone + Debug + PartialEq + Eq + core::hash::Hash + 'static {
    // ...
    fn data_attrs(&self) -> [(HtmlAttr, &'static str); 2] {
        [
            (HtmlAttr::Data("ars-scope"), Self::scope()),
            (HtmlAttr::Data("ars-part"), self.name()),
        ]
    }
}

        .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
        .set(part_attr, part_val)

This returns a **2-tuple array**: `[(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-scope"), "badge"), (HtmlAttr::Data("ars-part"), "root")]`

### 7. **Representative Stateless Component Test File: Badge**

**Full test module** (badge.rs lines 337-599):

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use ars_core::ConnectApi;
    use insta::assert_snapshot;

    use super::*;

    fn api(props: Props) -> Api {
        Api::new(props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default())
    }

    fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {
        format!("{attrs:#?}")
    }

    #[test]
    fn props_builder_chain_applies_each_setter() {
        let props = Props::new()
            .id("badge-1")
            .variant(Variant::Outline)
            .size(Size::Xl)
            .accessibility(Accessibility::Alert)
            .aria_label("3 unread messages");

        assert_eq!(props.id, "badge-1");
        assert_eq!(props.variant, Variant::Outline);
        assert_eq!(props.size, Size::Xl);
        assert_eq!(props.accessibility, Accessibility::Alert);
        assert_eq!(props.aria_label.as_deref(), Some("3 unread messages"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn badge_root_default_snapshot() {
        assert_snapshot!(
            "badge_root_default",
            snapshot_attrs(&api(Props::new()).root_attrs())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn badge_root_status_snapshot() {
        assert_snapshot!(
            "badge_root_status",
            snapshot_attrs(
                &api(Props::new()
                    .accessibility(Accessibility::Status)
                    .aria_label("3 unread notifications"))
                .root_attrs()
            )
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_attrs_set_status_live_and_label() {
        let attrs = api(Props::new()
            .accessibility(Accessibility::Status)
            .aria_label("3 unread messages"))
        .root_attrs();

        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("status"));
        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live)), Some("polite"));
        assert_eq!(
            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),
            Some("3 unread messages")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn part_attrs_delegates_to_root_attrs() {
        let api = api(Props::new().variant(Variant::Solid));

        assert_eq!(api.part_attrs(Part::Root), api.root_attrs());
    }
    // ... 20+ more tests covering enum variants, messages, edge cases ...
}

## Summary: Stateless Component Patterns in ars-components

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here are the exact patterns for stateless components in the ars-ui workspace:

---

### 1. MODULE STRUCTURE

**Directory layout:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/crates/ars-components/src/`

The codebase uses **directory-backed `mod.rs`** pattern with subdirectories owning child modules:

```
src/
├── data_display/
│   ├── mod.rs              (re-exports: avatar, badge, meter, progress, ...)
│   ├── avatar.rs           (stateful)
│   ├── badge.rs            (stateless)
│   ├── meter.rs            (stateless)
│   ├── skeleton.rs         (stateless)
│   ├── stat.rs             (stateless)
│   └── progress/           (directory-backed for stateful)
│       └── mod.rs
├── utility/
│   ├── mod.rs              (re-exports all utilities)
│   ├── separator/
│   │   ├── mod.rs          (directory-backed - stateless)
│   │   └── snapshots/
│   ├── visually_hidden/
│   │   ├── mod.rs          (directory-backed - stateless)
│   │   └── snapshots/
│   ├── button.rs           (stateful)
│   ├── client_only.rs      (stateless simple file)
│   └── ...
├── specialized/
│   ├── mod.rs
│   ├── angle_slider/       (directory-backed)
│   ├── color_swatch/       (directory-backed)
│   └── ...
└── lib.rs                  (top-level re-exports)
```

**Key files and their exact purposes:**

- `/crates/ars-components/src/lib.rs` (lines 1-50): Root crate re-exports all 7 main categories (`data_display`, `utility`, `input`, `overlay`, `layout`, `navigation`, `selection`, `specialized`)
- `/crates/ars-components/src/data_display/mod.rs` (lines 1-29): Re-exports badge, meter, skeleton, stat, avatar (and stateful progress, table, tag_group, rating_group)
- `/crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs` (lines 1-97): Re-exports all 30 utilities including `separator` (line 78) and `visually_hidden` (line 93)

---

### 2. CANONICAL STATELESS COMPONENT EXAMPLES

#### **Example A: Separator (Simplest - No Messages)**

**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/crates/ars-components/src/utility/separator/mod.rs` (445 lines)

Structure:
```rust
// Lines 15-28: Props
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, HasId)]
pub struct Props {
    pub id: String,
    pub orientation: Orientation,     // from ars_i18n
    pub decorative: bool,
}

// Lines 62-69: Part enum
#[derive(ComponentPart)]              // Derive macro
#[scope = "separator"]                 // Required attribute
pub enum Part {
    Root,
}

// Lines 75-162: Api implementation
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Api {
    props: Props,
}

impl Api {
    pub const fn new(props: Props) -> Self { ... }
    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
}

impl ConnectApi for Api {
    type Part = Part;
    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Self::Part) -> AttrMap { ... }
}
```

**Key characteristics:**
- No `Messages` struct (no i18n strings)
- `Api::new(props)` takes only `Props`, **not** `Env`
- Single part (`Root`)
- All builder setters are `const fn`

---

#### **Example B: Badge (With Messages & Env)**

**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/crates/ars-components/src/data_display/badge.rs` (599 lines)

Structure:
```rust
// Lines 9-11: Imports foundation types
use ars_core::{
    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, HtmlAttr, MessageFn,
};
use ars_i18n::{Locale, number};

// Lines 14-15: Type alias for message closure
type OverflowLabelFn = dyn Fn(u64, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync;
type BadgeLabelFn = dyn Fn(u64, &str, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync;

// Lines 18-47: Props with HasId derive
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]
pub struct Props {
    pub id: String,
    pub variant: Variant,
    pub size: Size,
    pub accessibility: Accessibility,
    pub aria_label: Option<String>,
}

impl Default for Props { ... }
impl Props {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self::default() }
    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self { ... }
    // ... more builder methods
}

// Lines 187-214: Messages struct
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Messages {
    pub overflow_label: MessageFn<OverflowLabelFn>,
    pub badge_label: MessageFn<BadgeLabelFn>,
}

impl Default for Messages {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            overflow_label: MessageFn::new(|count: u64, locale: &Locale| {
                format_count(count, locale)
            }),
            badge_label: MessageFn::new(
                |count: u64, category: &str, _locale: &Locale| match count {
                    0 => format!("No unread {category}s"),
                    1 => format!("1 unread {category}"),
                    n => format!("{n} unread {category}s"),
                },
            ),
        }
    }
}

impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}

// Lines 217-222: Part enum
#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "badge"]
pub enum Part {
    Root,
}

// Lines 225-249: Api with Messages field
pub struct Api {
    props: Props,
    locale: Locale,
    messages: Messages,
}

impl Debug for Api {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("badge::Api")
            .field("props", &self.props)
            .field("locale", &self.locale)
            .finish_non_exhaustive()  // Omit messages from Debug
    }
}

impl Api {
    /// Creates new API from Props + Env + Messages
    pub fn new(props: Props, env: &Env, messages: &Messages) -> Self {
        Self {
            props,
            locale: env.locale.clone(),
            messages: messages.clone(),
        }
    }
    
    // Helper methods that use messages
    pub fn overflow_label(&self, count: u64) -> String {
        (self.messages.overflow_label)(count, &self.locale)
    }
    
    // Root attrs with scope + part data attrs
    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
        
        attrs
            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
            .set(part_attr, part_val)
            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-variant"), self.props.variant.as_str())
            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-size"), self.props.size.as_str());
        
        // ... conditional accessibility attrs
        attrs
    }
}

impl ConnectApi for Api {
    type Part = Part;

    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Self::Part) -> AttrMap {
        match part {
            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
        }
    }
}
```

---

#### **Example C: Meter (Multiple Parts)**

**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/crates/ars-components/src/data_display/meter.rs` (720 lines)

Structure (lines 266-284, 287-469):
```rust
#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "meter"]
pub enum Part {
    Root,
    Label,
    Track,
    Range,
    ValueText,
}

pub struct Api {
    props: Props,
    locale: Locale,
    messages: Messages,
}

impl Api {
    pub fn new(props: Props, env: &Env, messages: &Messages) -> Self { ... }
    
    pub fn segment(&self) -> Segment { ... }  // Domain logic
    pub fn zone(&self) -> Zone { ... }        // Domain logic
    pub fn percent(&self) -> f64 { ... }      // Derived value
    pub fn value_text(&self) -> String { ... } // Uses messages
    
    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn track_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn range_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn value_text_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
}

impl ConnectApi for Api {
    type Part = Part;
    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Self::Part) -> AttrMap {
        match part {
            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
            Part::Track => self.track_attrs(),
            Part::Range => self.range_attrs(),
            Part::ValueText => self.value_text_attrs(),
        }
    }
}

// Helper to avoid duplication
fn part_attrs(part: &Part) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = part.data_attrs();
    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
    attrs
}
```

---

### 3. FOUNDATION TYPES & IMPORTS

**Core types used by all stateless components:**

| Type/Trait | Location | Import | Usage |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Props` | Component file | Define locally | Holds all configuration; derives `HasId` |
| `Part` enum | Component file | Define locally | Derives `#[derive(ComponentPart)]` with `#[scope = "..."]` |
| `Messages` struct | Component file | Define locally | `#[derive(Clone, Debug)]`, impl `ComponentMessages` |
| `Api` struct | Component file | Define locally | Holds Props + Locale + Messages; impl `ConnectApi` |
| `HasId` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::HasId` | Derive macro on `Props` to get `id(&self)`, `with_id()`, `set_id()` |
| `ComponentPart` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::ComponentPart` | Derive macro for `Part` enum; provides `scope()`, `name()`, `all()`, `data_attrs()` |
| `ComponentMessages` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::ComponentMessages` | Trait impl on `Messages` (marker trait requiring Clone + Default) |
| `ConnectApi` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::ConnectApi` | Trait impl on `Api`; type Part, fn part_attrs() |
| `MessageFn<T>` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::MessageFn` | Arc-wrapped closure; constructors: `::new(closure)`, `::static_str(&'static str)` |
| `AttrMap` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::AttrMap` | Methods: `.set(HtmlAttr, impl Into<AttrValue>)`, `.set_style(CssProperty, impl Into<String>)`, `.set_bool(HtmlAttr, bool)`, `.get(&HtmlAttr) -> Option<&str>`, `.contains(&HtmlAttr) -> bool` |
| `HtmlAttr` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::HtmlAttr` | Enum: `Data(&'static str)`, `Aria(AriaAttr)`, `Role`, `Id`, `Class`, `TabIndex`, etc. |
| `AriaAttr` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::AriaAttr` | Enum: `Hidden`, `Label`, `Live`, `Orientation`, `ValueNow`, `ValueMin`, `ValueMax`, `ValueText`, etc. |
| `CssProperty` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::CssProperty` | Typed CSS property enum for `.set_style()` |
| `Env` | `ars_core` | `use ars_core::Env` | Struct with `locale: Locale` and `intl_backend` fields |
| `Locale` | `ars_i18n` | `use ars_i18n::Locale` | Cloneable locale type; passed to message closures |

---

### 4. EXACT IMPORT PATTERNS FOR STATELESS COMPONENTS

**Badge imports (with Messages):**
```rust
use ars_core::{
    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, HtmlAttr, MessageFn,
};
use ars_i18n::{Locale, number};
```

**Separator imports (no Messages):**
```rust
use ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, HasId, HtmlAttr};
use ars_i18n::Orientation;
```

**Meter imports (with Messages & CssProperty):**
```rust
use ars_core::{
    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, CssProperty, Env, HtmlAttr,
    MessageFn,
};
use ars_i18n::{Locale, number};
```

**General pattern:**
```rust
use ars_core::{
    AriaAttr,           // ARIA attribute enum
    AttrMap,            // Attribute map with .set(), .set_style(), .get()
    ComponentMessages,  // Marker trait (if Messages struct needed)
    ComponentPart,      // Derive macro for Part enum
    ConnectApi,         // Trait impl for Api
    CssProperty,        // Typed CSS properties (if using .set_style())
    Env,                // Environment with locale (if Api needs Env)
    HtmlAttr,           // HTML attribute enum
    MessageFn,          // Arc-wrapped message closure (if Messages needed)
};
use ars_i18n::Locale;   // Locale type (if i18n messages)
```

---

### 5. RE-EXPORT WIRING

**Step 1: Category module (`src/data_display/mod.rs`):**
```rust
/// Badge component connect API.
pub mod badge;
```

**Step 2: Root crate (`src/lib.rs`):**
```rust
/// Data-display component machines.
pub mod data_display;
```

**Public usage from outside ars-components:**
```rust
use ars_components::data_display::badge::{Api, Props, Messages};
use ars_components::utility::separator::{Api, Props};
```

---

### 6. FOUNDATION TRAIT & MACRO DEFINITIONS

**`HasId` trait** (`ars_core/src/lib.rs:599-609`):
```rust
pub trait HasId: Sized {
    fn id(&self) -> &str;
    fn with_id(self, id: String) -> Self;
    fn set_id(&mut self, id: String);
}
```
Derived via `#[derive(HasId)]` on `Props`. Emits:
```rust
impl HasId for Props {
    fn id(&self) -> &str { &self.id }
    fn with_id(mut self, id: String) -> Self { self.id = id; self }
    fn set_id(&mut self, id: String) { self.id = id; }
}
```

**`ComponentPart` trait** (`ars_core/src/lib.rs:616-636`):
```rust
pub trait ComponentPart: Clone + Debug + PartialEq + Eq + core::hash::Hash + 'static {
    const ROOT: Self;
    fn scope() -> &'static str;
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str;
    fn all() -> Vec<Self>;
    fn data_attrs(&self) -> [(HtmlAttr, &'static str); 2];  // Returns ["ars-scope", "ars-part"] pair
}
```
Derived via `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = "component-name"]` on `enum Part`.

**`ComponentMessages` trait** (`ars_core/src/message_fn.rs:238-249`):
```rust
pub trait ComponentMessages: Clone + Default {}
```
Marker trait; always impl on component `Messages` structs.

**`ConnectApi` trait** (`ars_core/src/lib.rs:642-648`):
```rust
pub trait ConnectApi {
    type Part: ComponentPart;
    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Self::Part) -> AttrMap;
}
```
Always impl on component `Api` struct.

**`MessageFn` constructors** (`ars_core/src/message_fn.rs:54-236`):
```rust
// From closures (uses From impls for various arities)
pub fn new(f: impl Into<Self>) -> Self { f.into() }

// For static strings (ignores Locale parameter)
pub fn static_str(s: &'static str) -> Self
```

**`AttrMap` methods:**
- `pub fn new() -> Self` — create empty
- `pub fn set(&mut self, attr: HtmlAttr, value: impl Into<AttrValue>) -> &mut Self` — merge/replace
- `pub fn set_style(&mut self, prop: CssProperty, value: impl Into<String>) -> &mut Self`
- `pub fn set_bool(&mut self, attr: HtmlAttr, value: bool) -> &mut Self`
- `pub fn get(&self, attr: &HtmlAttr) -> Option<&str>`
- `pub fn contains(&self, attr: &HtmlAttr) -> bool`

---

### 7. COMMON PATTERNS IN ROOT_ATTRS()

**All stateless components follow this pattern:**

```rust
pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    
    // 1. Add scope + part data attrs (from ComponentPart::data_attrs())
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
    
    // 2. Add data-ars-* styling hooks
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-variant"), self.props.variant.as_str());
    
    // 3. Add role + aria-* semantics
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "status");
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), "polite");
    
    // 4. Add conditional attributes
    if let Some(label) = &self.props.aria_label {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), label.as_str());
    }
    
    attrs
}
```

### SUMMARY FOR YOUR QRCODE COMPONENT

For your stateless `QrCode` component, use this exact template:

**Location:** `/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/qr_code/mod.rs` or `/crates/ars-components/src/data_display/qr_code.rs`

**Minimal structure:**
```rust
use ars_core::{
    AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, HtmlAttr,
};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]
pub struct Props {
    pub id: String,
    pub value: String,
    pub size: u32,
}

impl Props {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self::default() }
    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self { /* ... */ }
}

#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "qr-code"]
pub enum Part {
    Root,
}

pub struct Api {
    props: Props,
}

impl Api {
    pub fn new(props: Props) -> Self { Self { props } }
    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
        attrs
    }
}

impl ConnectApi for Api {
    type Part = Part;
    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Self::Part) -> AttrMap {
        match part {
            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
        }
    }
}
```

**Then wire into module system:**
1. Add to `/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs`: `pub mod qr_code;`
2. Or add to `/crates/ars-components/src/data_display/mod.rs`: `pub mod qr_code;`
```

===== color_swatch id handling (stateless specialized) =====
    /// auto-generated perceptual name.
    #[must_use]
    pub fn color_name(&self) -> String {
        if let Some(name) = &self.props.color_name {
            name.clone()
        } else {
            let parts = self.props.color.color_name_parts();

            (self.messages.format_name)(&parts, &self.locale)
        }
    }

    /// Attributes for the root element.
    #[must_use]
    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();

        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);

        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {
            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());
        }

        attrs
            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, "img")
            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.color_name())
            .set(
                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::RoleDescription),
                (self.messages.role_description)(&self.locale),
            )
            .set_style(
                CssProperty::Custom("ars-swatch-color"),
                self.props.color.to_css_hsl(),
            );

        attrs
    }

    /// Attributes for the inner color-fill element.

===== color_swatch FULL =====
     1	//! `ColorSwatch` connect API.
     2	//!
     3	//! `ColorSwatch` is a stateless, non-interactive display element that renders a
     4	//! color preview with an accessible, perceptual color name for screen readers.
     5	//! No `Machine` is needed — it maps a [`ColorValue`] to attributes directly.
     6	//!
     7	//! The accessible name is derived from [`ColorValue::color_name_parts`] (which
     8	//! returns English keys) assembled by the [`Messages::format_name`] message, so
     9	//! the locale-appropriate ordering and token translations live in the i18n
    10	//! layer rather than in the color math.
    11	
    12	use alloc::{string::String, vec::Vec};
    13	
    14	use ars_core::{
    15	    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ColorNameParts, ColorValue, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi,
    16	    CssProperty, Env, HtmlAttr, MessageFn,
    17	};
    18	use ars_i18n::Locale;
    19	
    20	/// Formats [`ColorNameParts`] into a single localized accessible name.
    21	type FormatNameFn = dyn Fn(&ColorNameParts, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync;
    22	/// Returns the localized `aria-roledescription` for the swatch root.
    23	type RoleDescriptionFn = dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync;
    24	
    25	/// Props for the `ColorSwatch` component.
    26	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]
    27	pub struct Props {
    28	    /// Component instance ID. When non-empty it is emitted as the root `id`.
    29	    pub id: String,
    30	
    31	    /// The color to display.
    32	    pub color: ColorValue,
    33	
    34	    /// Optional override for the auto-generated accessible color name.
    35	    ///
    36	    /// When `None`, the name is derived from
    37	    /// [`ColorValue::color_name_parts`] + [`Messages::format_name`].
    38	    pub color_name: Option<String>,
    39	
    40	    /// Whether to visually represent the alpha channel (checkerboard pattern).
    41	    pub respect_alpha: bool,
    42	}
    43	
    44	impl Default for Props {
    45	    fn default() -> Self {
    46	        Self {
    47	            id: String::new(),
    48	            color: ColorValue::default(),
    49	            color_name: None,
    50	            respect_alpha: true,
    51	        }
    52	    }
    53	}
    54	
    55	/// Messages for the `ColorSwatch` component.
    56	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
    57	pub struct Messages {
    58	    /// Formats the color name parts into a localized string.
    59	    ///
    60	    /// Default (en): `"{lightness} {chroma} {hue}"` — non-empty parts joined by
    61	    /// spaces. Other locales may reorder the parts.
    62	    pub format_name: MessageFn<FormatNameFn>,
    63	
    64	    /// Role description for the swatch element (default: `"color swatch"`).
    65	    pub role_description: MessageFn<RoleDescriptionFn>,
    66	}
    67	
    68	impl Default for Messages {
    69	    fn default() -> Self {
    70	        Self {
    71	            format_name: MessageFn::new(|parts: &ColorNameParts, _locale: &Locale| {
    72	                [
    73	                    parts.lightness.as_str(),
    74	                    parts.chroma.as_str(),
    75	                    parts.hue.as_str(),
    76	                ]
    77	                .into_iter()
    78	                .filter(|part| !part.is_empty())
    79	                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    80	                .join(" ")
    81	            }),
    82	            role_description: MessageFn::static_str("color swatch"),
    83	        }
    84	    }
    85	}
    86	
    87	impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
    88	
    89	/// Structural parts exposed by the `ColorSwatch` connect API.
    90	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
    91	#[scope = "color-swatch"]
    92	pub enum Part {
    93	    /// Container with `role="img"` and the accessible color name.
    94	    Root,
    95	
    96	    /// Color fill element, with an optional checkerboard for alpha.
    97	    Inner,
    98	}
    99	
   100	/// API for the `ColorSwatch` component.
   101	#[derive(Debug)]
   102	pub struct Api<'a> {
   103	    props: &'a Props,
   104	    locale: Locale,
   105	    messages: Messages,
   106	}
   107	
   108	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
   109	    /// Creates a new API for the swatch.
   110	    #[must_use]
   111	    pub fn new(props: &'a Props, env: &Env, messages: &Messages) -> Self {
   112	        Self {
   113	            props,
   114	            locale: env.locale.clone(),
   115	            messages: messages.clone(),
   116	        }
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    /// The resolved accessible color name — either the explicit override or the
   120	    /// auto-generated perceptual name.
   121	    #[must_use]
   122	    pub fn color_name(&self) -> String {
   123	        if let Some(name) = &self.props.color_name {
   124	            name.clone()
   125	        } else {
   126	            let parts = self.props.color.color_name_parts();
   127	
   128	            (self.messages.format_name)(&parts, &self.locale)
   129	        }
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    /// Attributes for the root element.
   133	    #[must_use]
   134	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
   135	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
   136	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
   137	
   138	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
   139	
   140	        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {
   141	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());
   142	        }
   143	
   144	        attrs
   145	            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, "img")
   146	            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.color_name())
   147	            .set(
   148	                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::RoleDescription),
   149	                (self.messages.role_description)(&self.locale),
   150	            )
   151	            .set_style(
   152	                CssProperty::Custom("ars-swatch-color"),
   153	                self.props.color.to_css_hsl(),
   154	            );
   155	
   156	        attrs
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    /// Attributes for the inner color-fill element.
   160	    #[must_use]
   161	    pub fn inner_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
   162	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
   163	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Inner.data_attrs();
   164	
   165	        attrs
   166	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
   167	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
   168	            .set_style(CssProperty::Background, self.props.color.to_css_hsl());
   169	
   170	        if self.props.respect_alpha && self.props.color.alpha < 1.0 {
   171	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-alpha"), true);
   172	        }
   173	
   174	        attrs
   175	    }
   176	}
   177	
   178	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
   179	    type Part = Part;
   180	
   181	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
   182	        match part {
   183	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
   184	            Part::Inner => self.inner_attrs(),
   185	        }
   186	    }
   187	}

//! Each test pulls the expected anatomy from the corresponding component

use ars_components::specialized as specialized_core;

use super::helper::assert_anatomy;

I now have everything needed to design the plan. There's one genuine design fork that requires your input, because the spec text contradicts itself and the architecture.

**The contradiction:** The core spec's `Api::new` calls `QrMatrix::generate(value, error_correction)`, but (1) that method is never defined, (2) the `QrMatrix::new` doc comment says *"QR matrix generation is delegated to the `qrcode` crate in the adapter layer (`ars-dom`)"*, and (3) both adapter specs state *"The adapter owns encoding even though the core spec defines rendering semantics"* / *"rendering stays adapter-owned"*. On top of that, `ars-components` is `no_std`-by-default and the workspace has no QR crate, and new dependencies need your explicit approval.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.claude/plans/implement-task-288-floofy-codd.md",
  "content": "# Task #288 — Implement QrCode agnostic core\n\n## Context\n\nGitHub issue #288 (epic #227, Agnostic specialized components, 2 pts, Test tier: Unit) asks for the\n**framework-agnostic stateless core** of `QrCode` in `ars-components`: a declarative component that\nmaps an input value to a rendered QR matrix with accessible labeling, error-correction level, sizing,\ncolors, optional center overlay, and an optional download trigger. Framework adapters (Leptos/Dioxus)\nare explicitly out of scope.\n\nSpec: `spec/components/specialized/qr-code.md` (tier: stateless).\n\n### Resolved design tension (confirmed with user)\n\nThe core spec's `Api::new` calls `QrMatrix::generate(value, error_correction)`, but that method is\n**never defined**, the `QrMatrix::new` doc comment says *\"QR matrix generation is delegated to the\n`qrcode` crate in the adapter layer (`ars-dom`)\"*, and both adapter specs state *\"The adapter owns\nencoding even though the core spec defines rendering semantics.\"* `ars-components` is `no_std`-by-default\n(uses `alloc`), there is no QR crate in the workspace, and new deps require explicit approval.\n\nDecisions (both user-confirmed):\n1. **Inject the matrix.** The agnostic core defines only the *rendering contract*. `Api::new` accepts an\n   `Option<QrMatrix>` supplied by the caller (the adapter, later, generates it with `qrcode`). No\n   generation and no new dependency in core; stays `no_std`. This requires editing the core spec's\n   `Api::new` signature and removing the `QrMatrix::generate` call (spec-sync in this same PR).\n2. **Reinterpret the \"generates\" acceptance tests for core scope.** Cover `QrMatrix::new`/`get`/`size`\n   from a fixture grid, injected-matrix → `pixel_size` + width/height styles, and `QrErrorCorrection`'s\n   4 L/M/Q/H variants (Medium default) flowing through `Props`. Actual encoding is verified in the\n   future `ars-dom` adapter task (out of scope for #288).\n\n## Pattern to mirror\n\n`crates/ars-components/src/specialized/color_swatch/mod.rs` is the canonical analog: stateless\nspecialized component, `Api<'a>` borrowing `props: &'a Props`, `Api::new(props, env, messages)`,\n`role=\"img\"` + `aria-label`, the real id pattern (`if !props.id.is_empty() { set(Id, ...) }` — there is\n**no** `generate_id()` in the workspace), `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = \"...\"]`, `Messages` with\n`MessageFn` type-alias closures + `ComponentMessages`, inline `#[cfg(test)]` snapshot tests, and a\nsibling `snapshots/` directory.\n\n## Files to create / modify\n\n### 1. `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/qr_code/mod.rs` (new, directory-backed)\n\nImplement, matching spec §1 exactly except for the two confirmed deviations:\n\n- **Supporting types**\n  - `QrErrorCorrection` enum: `Low`, `Medium` (`#[default]`), `Quartile`, `High`; derive\n    `Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default`.\n  - `QrMatrix { modules: Vec<Vec<bool>>, size: usize }`; derive `Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq`\n    (add `PartialEq, Eq` vs spec's `Clone, Debug` for test ergonomics — sync into spec). `new(modules)`\n    sets `size = modules.len()`; `get(row, col) -> bool` keeps the spec's `debug_assert!` bounds check\n    and `.unwrap_or(false)` fallback.\n- **`Props`** — exact fields/defaults from spec §1.1: `id`, `value`, `error_correction`, `module_size: f64`,\n  `quiet_zone: usize`, `foreground`, `background`, `overlay_src: Option<String>`, `overlay_size: f64`.\n  Derive `Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId`; hand-written `Default` per spec. No builder methods\n  (spec defines none; color_swatch uses struct-literal). Default colors `#000000` / `#ffffff`.\n- **`Messages`** (spec §4.1) — `label` & `link_label` as `MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str,&Locale)->String+Send+Sync>`\n  (use a `LabelFn` type alias), `download_label` as `MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale)->String+Send+Sync>`\n  (`DownloadLabelFn` alias, built with `MessageFn::static_str`). `#[derive(Clone, Debug)]`; defaults\n  `\"QR code: {value}\"`, `\"QR code linking to {url}\"`, `\"Download QR code\"`; `impl ComponentMessages`.\n  (Verified: `From` impls exist for both `Fn(&str,&Locale)` and `Fn(&Locale)` arities; `static_str` is on\n  the `Fn(&Locale)` MessageFn.)\n- **`Part`** (spec §1.2): `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = \"qr-code\"]` with `Root, Frame, Pattern,\n  Overlay, DownloadTrigger`; doc comment on each variant (`missing_docs`).\n- **`Api<'a>`** — `{ props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>, locale: Locale, messages: Messages }`,\n  `#[derive(Debug)]` (matrix + messages are Debug). **Deviation 1:** drop spec's `id: String` field.\n  - `new(props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>, env: &Env, messages: &Messages)` — store injected\n    matrix; `locale = env.locale.clone()`; `messages = messages.clone()`. **No `QrMatrix::generate`,\n    no `generate_id`.**\n  - `matrix(&self) -> Option<&QrMatrix>`; `pixel_size(&self) -> f64` = `(m.size + quiet_zone*2) as f64 *\n    module_size` when matrix present, else `0.0`.\n  - `root_attrs`: scope/part; `if !props.id.is_empty() { set(Id, props.id.clone()) }` (**Deviation 2**,\n    mirrors color_swatch); `Role=\"img\"`; aria-label = `link_label` when `value` starts with `http://`/\n    `https://` else `label`; width/height styles `= format!(\"{px}px\", pixel_size())`.\n  - `frame_attrs`, `pattern_attrs`: scope/part only.\n  - `overlay_attrs`: scope/part + `Src` when `overlay_src` is `Some`.\n  - `download_trigger_attrs`: scope/part + `Type=\"button\"` + aria-label from `download_label`.\n  - `impl ConnectApi for Api<'_>` matching all 5 parts.\n- Imports: `use alloc::{format, string::String, vec::Vec};` and\n  `use ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, CssProperty, Env,\n  HtmlAttr, MessageFn};` + `use ars_i18n::Locale;`.\n\n### 2. `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs` (modify)\n\nAdd (alphabetically) `pub mod qr_code;` with a `/// QrCode stateless QR-matrix rendering connect API.`\ndoc line.\n\n### 3. `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs` (modify)\n\nAdd `qr_code_anatomy_matches_spec` calling `assert_anatomy(\"qr-code\", &[(Part::Root,\"root\"),\n(Part::Frame,\"frame\"),(Part::Pattern,\"pattern\"),(Part::Overlay,\"overlay\"),\n(Part::DownloadTrigger,\"download-trigger\")])` (heck kebab-cases `DownloadTrigger` → `download-trigger`).\n\n### 4. `spec/components/specialized/qr-code.md` (modify — spec-sync, same PR)\n\n- `QrMatrix` derive → add `PartialEq, Eq`.\n- `Api` struct: remove `id: String` field.\n- `Api::new`: signature → `new(props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>, env: &Env, messages: &Messages)`;\n  body stores the injected matrix; remove the `QrMatrix::generate` and `generate_id` lines.\n- `root_attrs`: replace `attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, &self.id)` with the `if !self.props.id.is_empty()` guard\n  using `self.props.id.clone()`.\n- Keep the `QrMatrix::new` doc comment (now accurate: generation is adapter-owned).\n\n## Tests (TDD — write first, in the inline `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`)\n\nHelpers `api(props, matrix)` + `snapshot_attrs` + a small `sample_matrix()` fixture (e.g. a 3×3 grid).\n\n- `connect_produces_img_role_with_aria_label` — `Role=\"img\"`, label `\"QR code: {value}\"`.\n- `url_value_uses_link_label` — `https://…` → `\"QR code linking to …\"`.\n- `error_correction_has_four_levels_with_medium_default` — `Default == Medium`; all 4 variants exist and\n  round-trip through `Props`.\n- `injected_matrix_drives_pixel_size_and_dimensions` — `pixel_size == (size + quiet_zone*2)*module_size`;\n  width/height styles equal `\"{px}px\"`.\n- `no_matrix_yields_zero_pixel_size` — `None` → `0.0`, `\"0px\"` dimensions.\n- `qr_matrix_new_computes_size_and_get` — `size == rows`; ## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Task #288 — Implement QrCode agnostic core

## Context

GitHub issue #288 (epic #227, Agnostic specialized components, 2 pts, Test tier: Unit) asks for the
**framework-agnostic stateless core** of `QrCode` in `ars-components`: a declarative component that
maps an input value to a rendered QR matrix with accessible labeling, error-correction level, sizing,
colors, optional center overlay, and an optional download trigger. Framework adapters (Leptos/Dioxus)
are explicitly out of scope.

Spec: `spec/components/specialized/qr-code.md` (tier: stateless).

### Resolved design tension (confirmed with user)

The core spec's `Api::new` calls `QrMatrix::generate(value, error_correction)`, but that method is
**never defined**, the `QrMatrix::new` doc comment says *"QR matrix generation is delegated to the
`qrcode` crate in the adapter layer (`ars-dom`)"*, and both adapter specs state *"The adapter owns
encoding even though the core spec defines rendering semantics."* `ars-components` is `no_std`-by-default
(uses `alloc`), there is no QR crate in the workspace, and new deps require explicit approval.

Decisions (both user-confirmed):
1. **Inject the matrix.** The agnostic core defines only the *rendering contract*. `Api::new` accepts an
   `Option<QrMatrix>` supplied by the caller (the adapter, later, generates it with `qrcode`). No
   generation and no new dependency in core; stays `no_std`. This requires editing the core spec's
   `Api::new` signature and removing the `QrMatrix::generate` call (spec-sync in this same PR).
2. **Reinterpret the "generates" acceptance tests for core scope.** Cover `QrMatrix::new`/`get`/`size`
   from a fixture grid, injected-matrix → `pixel_size` + width/height styles, and `QrErrorCorrection`'s
   4 L/M/Q/H variants (Medium default) flowing through `Props`. Actual encoding is verified in the
   future `ars-dom` adapter task (out of scope for #288).

## Pattern to mirror

`crates/ars-components/src/specialized/color_swatch/mod.rs` is the canonical analog: stateless
specialized component, `Api<'a>` borrowing `props: &'a Props`, `Api::new(props, env, messages)`,
`role="img"` + `aria-label`, the real id pattern (`if !props.id.is_empty() { set(Id, ...) }` — there is
**no** `generate_id()` in the workspace), `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = "..."]`, `Messages` with
`MessageFn` type-alias closures + `ComponentMessages`, inline `#[cfg(test)]` snapshot tests, and a
sibling `snapshots/` directory.

## Files to create / modify

### 1. `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/qr_code/mod.rs` (new, directory-backed)

Implement, matching spec §1 exactly except for the two confirmed deviations:

- **Supporting types**
  - `QrErrorCorrection` enum: `Low`, `Medium` (`#[default]`), `Quartile`, `High`; derive
    `Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default`.
  - `QrMatrix { modules: Vec<Vec<bool>>, size: usize }`; derive `Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq`
    (add `PartialEq, Eq` vs spec's `Clone, Debug` for test ergonomics — sync into spec). `new(modules)`
    sets `size = modules.len()`; `get(row, col) -> bool` keeps the spec's `debug_assert!` bounds check
    and `.unwrap_or(false)` fallback.
- **`Props`** — exact fields/defaults from spec §1.1: `id`, `value`, `error_correction`, `module_size: f64`,
  `quiet_zone: usize`, `foreground`, `background`, `overlay_src: Option<String>`, `overlay_size: f64`.
  Derive `Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId`; hand-written `Default` per spec. No builder methods
  (spec defines none; color_swatch uses struct-literal). Default colors `#000000` / `#ffffff`.
- **`Messages`** (spec §4.1) — `label` & `link_label` as `MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str,&Locale)->String+Send+Sync>`
  (use a `LabelFn` type alias), `download_label` as `MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale)->String+Send+Sync>`
  (`DownloadLabelFn` alias, built with `MessageFn::static_str`). `#[derive(Clone, Debug)]`; defaults
  `"QR code: {value}"`, `"QR code linking to {url}"`, `"Download QR code"`; `impl ComponentMessages`.
  (Verified: `From` impls exist for both `Fn(&str,&Locale)` and `Fn(&Locale)` arities; `static_str` is on
  the `Fn(&Locale)` MessageFn.)
- **`Part`** (spec §1.2): `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = "qr-code"]` with `Root, Frame, Pattern,
  Overlay, DownloadTrigger`; doc comment on each variant (`missing_docs`).
- **`Api<'a>`** — `{ props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>, locale: Locale, messages: Messages }`,
  `#[derive(Debug)]` (matrix + messages are Debug). **Deviation 1:** drop spec's `id: String` field.
  - `new(props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>, env: &Env, messages: &Messages)` — store injected
    matrix; `locale = env.locale.clone()`; `messages = messages.clone()`. **No `QrMatrix::generate`,
    no `generate_id`.**
  - `matrix(&self) -> Option<&QrMatrix>`; `pixel_size(&self) -> f64` = `(m.size + quiet_zone*2) as f64 *
    module_size` when matrix present, else `0.0`.
  - `root_attrs`: scope/part; `if !props.id.is_empty() { set(Id, props.id.clone()) }` (**Deviation 2**,
    mirrors color_swatch); `Role="img"`; aria-label = `link_label` when `value` starts with `http://`/
    `https://` else `label`; width/height styles `= format!("{px}px", pixel_size())`.
  - `frame_attrs`, `pattern_attrs`: scope/part only.
  - `overlay_attrs`: scope/part + `Src` when `overlay_src` is `Some`.
  - `download_trigger_attrs`: scope/part + `Type="button"` + aria-label from `download_label`.
  - `impl ConnectApi for Api<'_>` matching all 5 parts.
- Imports: `use alloc::{format, string::String, vec::Vec};` and
  `use ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, CssProperty, Env,
  HtmlAttr, MessageFn};` + `use ars_i18n::Locale;`.

### 2. `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs` (modify)

Add (alphabetically) `pub mod qr_code;` with a `/// QrCode stateless QR-matrix rendering connect API.`
doc line.

### 3. `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs` (modify)

Add `qr_code_anatomy_matches_spec` calling `assert_anatomy("qr-code", &[(Part::Root,"root"),
(Part::Frame,"frame"),(Part::Pattern,"pattern"),(Part::Overlay,"overlay"),
(Part::DownloadTrigger,"download-trigger")])` (heck kebab-cases `DownloadTrigger` → `download-trigger`).

### 4. `spec/components/specialized/qr-code.md` (modify — spec-sync, same PR)

- `QrMatrix` derive → add `PartialEq, Eq`.
- `Api` struct: remove `id: String` field.
- `Api::new`: signature → `new(props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>, env: &Env, messages: &Messages)`;
  body stores the injected matrix; remove the `QrMatrix::generate` and `generate_id` lines.
- `root_attrs`: replace `attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, &self.id)` with the `if !self.props.id.is_empty()` guard
  using `self.props.id.clone()`.
- Keep the `QrMatrix::new` doc comment (now accurate: generation is adapter-owned).

## Tests (TDD — write first, in the inline `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`)

Helpers `api(props, matrix)` + `snapshot_attrs` + a small `sample_matrix()` fixture (e.g. a 3×3 grid).

- `connect_produces_img_role_with_aria_label` — `Role="img"`, label `"QR code: {value}"`.
- `url_value_uses_link_label` — `https://…` → `"QR code linking to …"`.
- `error_correction_has_four_levels_with_medium_default` — `Default == Medium`; all 4 variants exist and
  round-trip through `Props`.
- `injected_matrix_drives_pixel_size_and_dimensions` — `pixel_size == (size + quiet_zone*2)*module_size`;
  width/height styles equal `"{px}px"`.
- `no_matrix_yields_zero_pixel_size` — `None` → `0.0`, `"0px"` dimensions.
- `qr_matrix_new_computes_size_and_get` — `size == rows`; `get` returns correct in-bounds modules (skip
  out-of-bounds, guarded by `debug_assert!`).
- `overlay_attrs_sets_src_only_when_present`.
- `download_trigger_sets_type_button_and_label`.
- `id_emitted_only_when_non_empty`.
- `color_props_default_and_override` — defaults `#000000`/`#ffffff`; overrides persist (colors are
  adapter-rendered, not emitted as core attrs).
- `part_attrs_delegates` — each `Part` equals its dedicated `*_attrs()`.
- **Snapshot tests** (`assert_snapshot!`, committed `.snap` in `specialized/qr_code/snapshots/`) covering
  every part and each output-affecting branch: root (default), root (URL + custom size + id), frame,
  pattern, overlay (with `overlay_src`), download_trigger, and a no-matrix root.

## Verification

1. `cargo test -p ars-components qr_code` (unit + snapshots).
2. `cargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject`, then review/accept new `.snap` fixtures.
3. `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance qr_code`.
4. `cargo clippy -p ars-components --all-targets` — zero warnings (`missing_docs`, workspace lints).
5. **MANDATORY** `post-implementation-audit` skill (spec/impl drift, "anything missing" rounds, coverage
   audit) — land every finding in this PR.
6. After user approval: `cargo xci-fast`; commit; PR targeting `main` with `Closes #288`; add
   `snapshot-reviewed` label (PR adds `.snap` files); then `waiting-for-codex-review` skill loop.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/qr_code/mod.rs",
  "content": "//! `QrCode` connect API.\n//!\n//! `QrCode` is a stateless, declarative component that maps an input value to a\n//! rendered QR code matrix with an accessible label, optional center overlay,\n//! and an optional download trigger. No `Machine` is needed.\n//!\n//! QR matrix generation is **adapter-owned**: the agnostic core defines only the\n//! rendering contract ([`QrMatrix`], [`Api`], [`Part`]). The matrix is supplied\n//! to [`Api::new`] by the caller (the framework adapter encodes the value with a\n//! QR library and injects the result). This keeps the core dependency-free and\n//! `no_std`-compatible while letting adapters own encoding and download export.\n\nuse alloc::{format, string::String, vec::Vec};\n\nuse ars_core::{\n    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, CssProperty, Env, HtmlAttr,\n    MessageFn,\n};\nuse ars_i18n::Locale;\n\n/// Formats the root `aria-label` from the encoded value (default\n/// `\"QR code: {value}\"`, or a link-specific variant for URLs).\ntype LabelFn = dyn Fn(&str, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync;\n/// Returns the localized `aria-label` for the download trigger.\ntype DownloadLabelFn = dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync;\n\n/// Error correction level for QR encoding.\n///\n/// Higher levels recover from more damage at the cost of denser matrices.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]\npub enum QrErrorCorrection {\n    /// ~7% recovery.\n    Low,\n    /// ~15% recovery.\n    #[default]\n    Medium,\n    /// ~25% recovery.\n    Quartile,\n    /// ~30% recovery.\n    High,\n}\n\n/// The QR code matrix — a 2D grid of modules (dark/light cells).\n///\n/// The agnostic core does not generate matrices; it renders them. Adapters\n/// encode the value with a QR library and construct a `QrMatrix` via\n/// [`QrMatrix::new`].\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct QrMatrix {\n    /// Row-major module data. `true` = dark module.\n    pub modules: Vec<Vec<bool>>,\n    /// Size (number of modules per side).\n    pub size: usize,\n}\n\nimpl QrMatrix {\n    /// Create a `QrMatrix` from a pre-computed module grid.\n    ///\n    /// QR matrix generation is delegated to a QR encoding crate in the adapter\n    /// layer. The core defines the rendering contract only.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn new(modules: Vec<Vec<bool>>) -> Self {\n        let size = modules.len();\n        Self { modules, size }\n    }\n\n    /// Get the module value at `(row, col)`.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `false` for out-of-bounds coordinates; a `debug_assert!` flags\n    /// such access during development.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn get(&self, row: usize, col: usize) -> bool {\n        debug_assert!(\n            row < self.size && col < self.size,\n            \"QrMatrix::get() out of bounds: ({row}, {col}) for size {}\",\n            self.size\n        );\n        self.modules\n            .get(row)\n            .and_then(|cells| cells.get(col))\n            .copied()\n            .unwrap_or(false)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Props for the `QrCode` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]\npub struct Props {\n    /// Component instance ID. When non-empty it is emitted as the root `id`.\n    pub id: String,\n    /// The data to encode.\n    pub value: String,\n    /// Error correction level.\n    pub error_correction: QrErrorCorrection,\n    /// Size of each module in pixels (for rendering).\n    pub module_size: f64,\n    /// Quiet zone (border) in modules. Standard is 4.\n    pub quiet_zone: usize,\n    /// Foreground color (dark modules).\n    pub foreground: String,\n    /// Background color (light modules).\n    pub background: String,\n    /// Optional image overlay (e.g., logo) in the center.\n    pub overlay_src: Option<String>,\n    /// Overlay size as a fraction of the QR code size, in `[0.0, 0.5]`.\n    pub overlay_size: f64,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Props {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: String::new(),\n            value: String::new(),\n            error_correction: QrErrorCorrection::Medium,\n            module_size: 4.0,\n            quiet_zone: 4,\n            foreground: \"#000000\".into(),\n            background: \"#ffffff\".into(),\n            overlay_src: None,\n            overlay_size: 0.2,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Messages for the `QrCode` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug)]\npub struct Messages {\n    /// Root `aria-label` template (default: `\"QR code: {value}\"`).\n    pub label: MessageFn<LabelFn>,\n    /// Label when the value is a URL (default: `\"QR code linking to {url}\"`).\n    pub link_label: MessageFn<LabelFn>,\n    /// Label for the download trigger button (default: `\"Download QR code\"`).\n    pub download_label: MessageFn<DownloadLabelFn>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Messages {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            label: MessageFn::new(|value: &str, _locale: &Locale| format!(\"QR code: {value}\")),\n            link_label: MessageFn::new(|url: &str, _locale: &Locale| {\n                format!(\"QR code linking to {url}\")\n            }),\n            download_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Download QR code\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ComponentMessages for Messages {}\n\n/// Structural parts exposed by the `QrCode` connect API.\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]\n#[scope = \"qr-code\"]\npub enum Part {\n    /// Container with `role=\"img\"`, sized to the rendered QR code.\n    Root,\n    /// Optional decorative frame around the code.\n    Frame,\n    /// The QR module grid, rendered as SVG or canvas by the adapter.\n    Pattern,\n    /// Optional centered image/logo overlay.\n    Overlay,\n    /// Optional button to download the QR code as an image.\n    DownloadTrigger,\n}\n\n/// API for the `QrCode` component.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct Api<'a> {\n    props: &'a Props,\n    matrix: Option<QrMatrix>,\n    locale: Locale,\n    messages: Messages,\n}\n\nimpl<'a> Api<'a> {\n    /// Creates a new API from props, an (adapter-supplied) matrix, env, and\n    /// messages.\n    ///\n    /// The `matrix` is `None` until the adapter has encoded `props.value`; the\n    /// core renders whatever it is given.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn new(\n        props: &'a Props,\n        matrix: Option<QrMatrix>,\n        env: &Env,\n        messages: &Messages,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            props,\n            matrix,\n            locale: env.locale.clone(),\n            messages: messages.clone(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// The injected QR matrix, if one has been supplied.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn matrix(&self) -> Option<&QrMatrix> {\n        self.matrix.as_ref()\n    }\n\n    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including the quiet zone).\n    ///\n    /// Returns `0.0` when no matrix has been supplied.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        match &self.matrix {\n            Some(matrix) => {\n                (matrix.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.props.module_size\n            }\n            None => 0.0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the root element.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);\n\n        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());\n        }\n\n        let label = if self.props.value.starts_with(\"http://\")\n            || self.props.value.starts_with(\"https://\")\n        {\n            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        } else {\n            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        };\n\n        let size = self.pixel_size();\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"img\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), label)\n            .set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!(\"{size}px\"))\n            .set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!(\"{size}px\"));\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the optional decorative frame.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn frame_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        part_attrs(&Part::Frame)\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the QR module grid.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pattern_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        part_attrs(&Part::Pattern)\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the optional centered overlay image.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn overlay_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = part_attrs(&Part::Overlay);\n        if let Some(src) = &self.props.overlay_src {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Src, src.clone());\n        }\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the optional download trigger button.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn download_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = part_attrs(&Part::DownloadTrigger);\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"button\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.messages.download_label)(&self.locale),\n            );\n        attrs\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {\n    type Part = Part;\n\n    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {\n        match part {\n            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),\n            Part::Frame => self.frame_attrs(),\n            Part::Pattern => self.pattern_attrs(),\n            Part::Overlay => self.overlay_attrs(),\n            Part::DownloadTrigger => self.download_trigger_attrs(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Builds an [`AttrMap`] seeded with a part's `data-ars-scope`/`data-ars-part`.\nfn part_attrs(part: &Part) -> AttrMap {\n    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = part.data_attrs();\n    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);\n    attrs\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use alloc::{string::ToString, vec};\n\n    use ars_core::ConnectApi;\n    use insta::assert_snapshot;\n\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn api<'a>(props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>) -> Api<'a> {\n        Api::new(props, matrix, &Env::default(), &Messages::default())\n    }\n\n    fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {\n        alloc::format!(\"{attrs:#?}\")\n    }\n\n    /// A small fixture grid standing in for an encoded matrix. The core does not\n    /// validate QR semantics, so any square grid exercises the rendering path.\n    fn sample_matrix() -> QrMatrix {\n        QrMatrix::new(vec![\n            vec![true, false, true],\n            vec![false, true, false],\n            vec![true, false, true],\n        ])\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn connect_produces_img_role_with_aria_label() {\n        let props = Props {\n            value: \"hello\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n\n        let attrs = api(&props, Some(sample_matrix())).root_attrs();\n\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"img\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"QR code: hello\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn url_value_uses_link_label() {\n        let https = Props {\n            value: \"https://example.com\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        assert_eq!(\n            api(&https, Some(sample_matrix()))\n                .root_attrs()\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"QR code linking to https://example.com\")\n        );\n\n        let http = Props {\n            value: \"http://example.com\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        assert_eq!(\n            api(&http, Some(sample_matrix()))\n                .root_attrs()\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"QR code linking to http://example.com\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn error_correction_has_four_levels_with_medium_default() {\n        assert_eq!(QrErrorCorrection::default(), QrErrorCorrection::Medium);\n\n        for level in [\n            QrErrorCorrection::Low,\n            QrErrorCorrection::Medium,\n            QrErrorCorrection::Quartile,\n            QrErrorCorrection::High,\n        ] {\n            let props = Props {\n                error_correction: level,\n                ..Props::default()\n            };\n            assert_eq!(props.error_correction, level);\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn injected_matrix_drives_pixel_size_and_dimensions() {\n        let props = Props {\n            module_size: 5.0,\n            quiet_zone: 2,\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n\n        let connected = api(&props, Some(sample_matrix()));\n\n        // (size 3 + quiet_zone 2 * 2) * module_size 5 = 35\n        assert!((connected.pixel_size() - 35.0).abs() < f64::EPSILON);\n\n        let attrs = connected.root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(\n            attrs.styles().iter().find(|(p, _)| *p == CssProperty::Width),\n            Some(&(CssProperty::Width, \"35px\".to_string()))\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            attrs\n                .styles()\n                .iter()\n                .find(|(p, _)| *p == CssProperty::Height),\n            Some(&(CssProperty::Height, \"35px\".to_string()))\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn no_matrix_yields_zero_pixel_size() {\n        let props = Props::default();\n        let connected = api(&props, None);\n\n        assert_eq!(connected.matrix(), None);\n        assert!((connected.pixel_size() - 0.0).abs() < f64::EPSILON);\n\n        let attrs = connected.root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(\n            attrs.styles().iter().find(|(p, _)| *p == CssProperty::Width),\n            Some(&(CssProperty::Width, \"0px\".to_string()))\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn qr_matrix_new_computes_size_and_get() {\n        let matrix = sample_matrix();\n\n        assert_eq!(matrix.size, 3);\n        assert!(matrix.get(0, 0));\n        assert!(!matrix.get(0, 1));\n        assert!(matrix.get(1, 1));\n        assert!(!matrix.get(2, 1));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn overlay_attrs_sets_src_only_when_present() {\n        let without = Props::default();\n        assert!(!api(&without, None).overlay_attrs().contains(&HtmlAttr::Src));\n\n        let with = Props {\n            overlay_src: Some(\"/logo.png\".to_string()),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        assert_eq!(\n            api(&with, None).overlay_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Src),\n            Some(\"/logo.png\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn download_trigger_sets_type_button_and_label() {\n        let props = Props::default();\n        let attrs = api(&props, None).download_trigger_attrs();\n\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Type), Some(\"button\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"Download QR code\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn id_emitted_only_when_non_empty() {\n        let without = Props::default();\n        assert!(!api(&without, None).root_attrs().contains(&HtmlAttr::Id));\n\n        let with = Props {\n            id: \"qr-1\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        assert_eq!(\n            api(&with, None).root_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Id),\n            Some(\"qr-1\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn color_props_default_and_override() {\n        let defaults = Props::default();\n        assert_eq!(defaults.foreground, \"#000000\");\n        assert_eq!(defaults.background, \"#ffffff\");\n\n        let custom = Props {\n            foreground: \"#112233\".to_string(),\n            background: \"#fefefe\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        assert_eq!(custom.foreground, \"#112233\");\n        assert_eq!(custom.background, \"#fefefe\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn part_attrs_delegates() {\n        let props = Props {\n            id: \"qr-1\".to_string(),\n            value: \"hello\".to_string(),\n            overlay_src: Some(\"/logo.png\".to_string()),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        let connected = api(&props, Some(sample_matrix()));\n\n        assert_eq!(connected.part_attrs(Part::Root), connected.root_attrs());\n        assert_eq!(connected.part_attrs(Part::Frame), connected.frame_attrs());\n        assert_eq!(\n            connected.part_attrs(Part::Pattern),\n            connected.pattern_attrs()\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            connected.part_attrs(Part::Overlay),\n            connected.overlay_attrs()\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            connected.part_attrs(Part::DownloadTrigger),\n            connected.download_trigger_attrs()\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_default_snapshot() {\n        let props = Props {\n            value: \"hello\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        assert_snapshot!(\n            \"qr_code_root_default\",\n            snapshot_attrs(&api(&props, Some(sample_matrix())).root_attrs())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_url_with_id_snapshot() {\n        let props = Props {\n            id: \"qr-1\".to_string(),\n            value: \"https://example.com\".to_string(),\n            module_size: Some(sample_matrix())).root_attrs())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_no_matrix_snapshot() {\n        let props = Props {\n            value: \"hello\".to_string(),\n fn frame_snapshot() {\n        let props = Props::default();\n        assert_snapshot!(\n            \"qr_code_frame\",\n            snapshot_attrs(&api(&props, None).frame_attrs())\n        );\n
