{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/spec/components/specialized/qr-code.md",
  "old_string": "pub struct Api<'a> {\n    props: &'a Props,\n    matrix: Option<QrMatrix>,\n    id: String,\n    locale: Locale,\n    messages: Messages,\n}\n\nimpl<'a> Api<'a> {\n    pub fn new(props: &'a Props, env: &Env, messages: &Messages) -> Self {\n        let matrix = QrMatrix::generate(&props.value, props.error_correction);\n        let id = if props.id.is_empty() { generate_id() } else { props.id.clone() };\n        let locale = env.locale.clone();\n        let messages = messages.clone();\n        Self { props, matrix, id, locale, messages }\n    }\n\n    pub fn matrix(&self) -> Option<&QrMatrix> {\n        self.matrix.as_ref()\n    }",
  "new_string": "pub struct Api<'a> {\n    props: &'a Props,\n    matrix: Option<QrMatrix>,\n    locale: Locale,\n    messages: Messages,\n}\n\nimpl<'a> Api<'a> {\n    /// QR matrix generation is adapter-owned: the agnostic core defines only the\n    /// rendering contract, so the caller (the framework adapter) encodes\n    /// `props.value` and injects the resulting `matrix`. It is `None` until the\n    /// adapter has produced it; the core renders whatever it is given.\n    pub fn new(props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>, env: &Env, messages: &Messages) -> Self {\n        let locale = env.locale.clone();\n        let messages = messages.clone();\n        Self { props, matrix, locale, messages }\n    }\n\n    pub const fn matrix(&self) -> Option<&QrMatrix> {\n        self.matrix.as_ref()\n    }"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288/spec/components/specialized/qr-code.md",
  "old_string": "    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including quiet zone).\n    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        if let Some(ref m) = self.matrix {\n            (m.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.props.module_size\n        } else {\n            0.0\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, &self.id);\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"img\");\n        let label = if self.props.value.starts_with(\"http://\") || self.props.value.starts_with(\"https://\") {\n            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        } else {\n            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), label);\n        let size = self.pixel_size();\n        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!(\"{}px\", size));\n        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!(\"{}px\", size));\n        attrs\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including quiet zone).\n    /// Returns `0.0` when no matrix has been supplied.\n    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        match &self.matrix {\n            Some(matrix) => (matrix.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.props.module_size,\n            None => 0.0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        // The id is adapter-supplied; emit it only when set (no in-core id generation).\n        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());\n        }\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"img\");\n        let label = if self.props.value.starts_with(\"http://\") || self.props.value.starts_with(\"https://\") {\n            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        } else {\n            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), label);\n        let size = self.pixel_size();\n        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!(\"{size}px\"));\n        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!(\"{size}px\"));\n        attrs\n    }"
}

===== LEPTOS 165-185 =====

Leptos can render the QR pattern as stable SVG children and wire the download trigger through a client-only click handler without additional reactive state.

## 24. Canonical Implementation Sketch

```rust
#[component]
pub fn QrCode(value: String) -> impl IntoView {
    let api = qr_code::Api::new(&qr_code::Props { value, ..Default::default() });
    view! {
        <div {..api.root_attrs()}>
            <svg {..api.pattern_attrs()} />
        </div>
    }
}
```

## 25. Reference Implementation Skeleton

- Encode the current value through the QR helper.
- Render the semantic root and vector pattern.
===== DIOXUS 180-200 =====

```rust
#[derive(Props, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct QrCodeSketchProps {
    pub value: String,
}

#[component]
pub fn QrCode(props: QrCodeSketchProps) -> Element {
    let api = qr_code::Api::new(&qr_code::Props { value: props.value, ..Default::default() });
    rsx! {
        div { ..api.root_attrs(),
            svg { ..api.pattern_attrs() }
        }
    }
}
```

## 25. Reference Implementation Skeleton

- Encode the current value through the QR helper.

66	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]
67	pub struct Props {
68	    /// Component instance ID.
69	    pub id: String,
70	    /// The data to encode.
71	    pub value: String,
72	    /// Error correction level.
73	    pub error_correction: QrErrorCorrection,
74	    /// Size of each module in pixels (for rendering).
75	    pub module_size: f64,
76	    /// Quiet zone (border) in modules. Standard is 4.
77	    pub quiet_zone: usize,
78	    /// Foreground color (dark modules).
79	    pub foreground: String,
80	    /// Background color (light modules).
81	    pub background: String,
82	    /// Optional image overlay (e.g., logo) in the center.
83	    pub overlay_src: Option<String>,
84	    /// Overlay size as a fraction of the QR code size [0.0, 0.5].
85	    pub overlay_size: f64,
86	}
87

90	        Self {
91	            id: String::new(),
92	            value: String::new(),
93	            error_correction: QrErrorCorrection::Medium,
94	            module_size: 4.0,
95	            quiet_zone: 4,
96	            foreground: "#000000".into(),

104	
105	### 1.2 Connect / API
106	
107	```rust
108	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
109	#[scope = "qr-code"]
110	pub enum Part {
111	    Root,
112	    Frame,
113	    Pattern,
114	    Overlay,
115	    DownloadTrigger,
116	}
117	
118	pub struct Api<'a> {
119	    props: &'a Props,
120	    matrix: Option<QrMatrix>,
121	    locale: Locale,
122	    messages: Messages,
123	}
124	
125	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
126	    /// QR matrix generation is adapter-owned: the agnostic core defines only the
127	    /// rendering contract, so the caller (the framework adapter) encodes
128	    /// `props.value` and injects the resulting `matrix`. It is `None` until the
129	    /// adapter has produced it; the core renders whatever it is given.
130	    pub fn new(props: &'a Props, matrix: Option<QrMatrix>, env: &Env, messages: &Messages) -> Self {
131	        let locale = env.locale.clone();
132	        let messages = messages.clone();
133	        Self { props, matrix, locale, messages }
134	    }
135	
136	    pub const fn matrix(&self) -> Option<&QrMatrix> {
137	        self.matrix.as_ref()
138	    }
139	
140	    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including quiet zone).
141	    /// Returns `0.0` when no matrix has been supplied.
142	    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {
143	        match &self.matrix {
144	            Some(matrix) => (matrix.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.props.module_size,
145	            None => 0.0,
146	        }
147	    }
148	
149	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
150	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
151	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
152	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
153	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
154	        // The id is adapter-supplied; emit it only when set (no in-core id generation).
155	        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {
156	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());
157	        }
158	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "img");
159	        let label = if self.props.value.starts_with("http://") || self.props.value.starts_with("https://") {
160	            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)
161	        } else {
162	            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)
163	        };
164	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), label);
165	        let size = self.pixel_size();
166	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!("{size}px"));
167	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!("{size}px"));
168	        attrs
169	    }
170	
171	    pub fn frame_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
172	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
173	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Frame.data_attrs();
174	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
175	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
176	        attrs
177	    }
178	
179	    pub fn pattern_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
180	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
181	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Pattern.data_attrs();
182	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
183	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
184	        attrs
185	    }
186	
187	    pub fn overlay_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
188	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
189	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Overlay.data_attrs();
190	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
191	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
192	        if let Some(src) = &self.props.overlay_src {
193	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Src, src.clone());
194	        }
195	        attrs
196	    }
197	
198	    pub fn download_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
199	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
200	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::DownloadTrigger.data_attrs();
201	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
202	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
203	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button");
204	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.messages.download_label)(&self.locale));
205	        attrs
206	    }
207	}
208	
209	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
210	    type Part = Part;
211	
212	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
213	        match part {
214	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
215	            Part::Frame => self.frame_attrs(),
216	            Part::Pattern => self.pattern_attrs(),
217	            Part::Overlay => self.overlay_attrs(),
218	            Part::DownloadTrigger => self.download_trigger_attrs(),
219	        }
```

Doctests are inconsistent here — the closest stateless peers (`color_swatch`, `badge`, `meter`, `separator`) carry **zero** doctests, so it's not a blanket convention. But QrCode has one genuinely non-obvious aspect that those peers lack: the **matrix-injection contract** (you must supply the matrix; the core won't generate it). That's exactly what an executable example should document. Let me check how existing doctests in the crate are written so I mirror the pattern.

> AGENT

Implementation and the mandatory 3-phase audit are complete. Here is the result for your review.

## Task #288 — QrCode agnostic core

### What was built
A stateless `QrCode` connect API in `ars-components`, mirroring the `color_swatch` pattern.

**New:** [crates/ars-components/src/specialized/qr_code/mod.rs](crates/ars-components/src/specialized/qr_code/mod.rs)
- `QrErrorCorrection` (Low/Medium/Quartile/High, Medium default), `QrMatrix` (`new`/`get`/`size`, rendering contract only).
- `Props` (exact spec §1.1 fields/defaults), `Messages` (`label`/`link_label`/`download_label`), `Part` (`#[scope="qr-code"]`: Root, Frame, Pattern, Overlay, DownloadTrigger).
- `Api<'a>` with injected `Option<QrMatrix>`: `root_attrs` (role=img, URL-aware aria-label, pixel-sized width/height), `frame`/`pattern`/`overlay` (Src when present)/`download_trigger` (type=button) attrs, plus `ConnectApi`.

**Modified:** `specialized/mod.rs` (module wired in), `tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs` (anatomy test), and **spec sync** in `spec/components/specialized/qr-code.md` + both adapter sketches (`Api::new` now takes the injected `matrix`; removed the undefined `QrMatrix::generate`/`generate_id`).

### Audit findings landed (no deferral)
- **Phase 1 (drift):** spec was synced in-task → spec and impl match exactly. No fabricated findings.
- **Phase 2 (anything missing):** **F1** — added a `# Examples` doctest on `QrMatrix` documenting the non-obvious matrix-injection contract. (No feature wiring / prelude / manifest / catalog changes needed; no new deps.)
- **Phase 3 (coverage):** `qr_code/mod.rs` has **0 uncovered lines**. **F2** — added the `Overlay`-without-`src` snapshot so every output-affecting branch has a fixture. The `QrMatrix::get` `.unwrap_or(false)` is a release-build defensive guard (kept).

### Verification
| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| `cargo test -p ars-components --all-targets` | 4350 + 109 spec-conformance pass |
| Doctests | 22 pass (incl. new qr_code) |
| `cargo clippy -p ars-components --all-targets --all-features` | clean |
| `cargo doc -p ars-components` | no new qr_code warnings |
| Spec snippet compile (`xtask`) | pass |
| `cargo xtask spec validate` | 340 files OK |
| Snapshot orphan check (`--features i18n --unreferenced=reject`) | no unreferenced |

**12 unit/logic tests + 8 snapshots + 1 doctest + 1 spec-conformance test.** Three design points to flag for your review:
1. **Injected matrix** (your confirmed choice): the core never encodes; `Api::new(props, matrix, env, messages)`. This required the spec-signature edit.
2. **`foreground`/`background`/`overlay_size` props are stored but not emitted as core attrs** — they feed adapter-owned Pattern/Overlay rendering, matching the spec (which emits no color attrs on root).
3. **Adapter sketches** reference an illustrative `encode_matrix(...)`/`&env`/`&messages` (loose pseudo-code, consistent with the existing `color_swatch` sketch convention).

May I proceed to run `cargo xci-fast` and then commit + open the PR (`Closes #288`)?

use super::*;

proptest! {
    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]

    /// Same dispatch invariant for Separator.
    #[test]
    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
    fn proptest_separator_part_root_dispatch_equals_root_attrs(
        props in arb_separator_props(),
    ) {
        let api = utility_core::separator::Api::new(props);

        prop_assert_eq!(api.part_attrs(utility_core::separator::Part::Root), api.root_attrs());
    }

    /// Same scope/part contract for Separator.
    #[test]
    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
    fn proptest_separator_root_attrs_always_have_scope_and_part(
        props in arb_separator_props(),
    ) {
        let attrs = utility_core::separator::Api::new(props).root_attrs();

        prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-scope")), Some("separator"));
        prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-part")), Some("root"));
    }

    /// Decorative separators carry `role="none"` and omit both
    /// `aria-orientation` and the `data-ars-orientation` styling hook.
    /// Semantic separators carry `role="separator"` plus `aria-orientation`
    /// and `data-ars-orientation` matching the layout axis. Pins F3/F9.
    #[test]
    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
    fn proptest_separator_decorative_branch_invariants(
        props in arb_separator_props(),
    ) {
        let decorative = props.decorative;

        let orientation = props.orientation;

        let attrs = utility_core::separator::Api::new(props).root_attrs();

        if decorative {
            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("none"));
            prop_assert_eq!(
                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(ars_core::AriaAttr::Orientation)),
                None
            );
            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-orientation")), None);
            prop_assert_eq!(
                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(ars_core::AriaAttr::Hidden)),
                None
            );
        } else {
            let expected = match orientation {
                Orientation::Horizontal => "horizontal",
                Orientation::Vertical => "vertical",
            };

            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("separator"));
            prop_assert_eq!(
                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(ars_core::AriaAttr::Orientation)),
                Some(expected)
            );
            prop_assert_eq!(
                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-orientation")),
                Some(expected)
            );
        }
    }

    /// For decorative separators, `orientation` is invisible to the
    /// agnostic-core output. Pins the "decorative collapses orientation"
    /// invariant tested under one example in the unit suite.
    #[test]
    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
    fn proptest_separator_decorative_orientation_does_not_affect_output(
        id in arb_short_id(),
    ) {
        let h = utility_core::separator::Api::new(utility_core::separator::Props {
            id: id.clone(),
            orientation: Orientation::Horizontal,
            decorative: true,
        })
        .root_attrs();

        let v = utility_core::separator::Api::new(utility_core::separator::Props {
            id,
            orientation: Orientation::Vertical,
            decorative: true,
        })
        .root_attrs();

        prop_assert_eq!(h, v);
    }

    /// `Api::props()` round-trip for Separator.
    #[test]
    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
    fn proptest_separator_api_props_round_trip(
        props in arb_separator_props(),
    ) {
        let original = props.clone();

        let api = utility_core::separator::Api::new(props);

        prop_assert_eq!(api.props(), &original);
        prop_assert_eq!(api.id(), original.id.as_str());
        prop_assert_eq!(api.orientation(), original.orientation);
        prop_assert_eq!(api.decorative(), original.decorative);
    }
}
```

//! Property-based state-machine tests for the specialized color components.
//!
//! Each block feeds an arbitrary event sequence through a machine and asserts
//! the component's core invariants always hold: stored values stay in range,
//! states stay within the declared set, and `connect()` never panics. Run in
//! the nightly `extended-proptest` job; `#[ignore]`d in the fast tier.

// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ColorArea
// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

mod color_area_proptests {
    use ars_components::specialized::color_area::{Event, Machine, Props};
    use ars_core::{AriaAttr, ColorChannel, Env, HtmlAttr, Service};
    use proptest::prelude::*;

    fn arb_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {
        prop_oneof![
            (0.0f64..1.0, 0.0f64..1.0).prop_map(|(x, y)| Event::DragStart { x, y }),
            (0.0f64..1.0, 0.0f64..1.0).prop_map(|(x, y)| Event::DragMove { x, y }),
            Just(Event::DragEnd),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::IncrementX { step }),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::DecrementX { step }),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::IncrementY { step }),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::DecrementY { step }),
            Just(Event::SetXToMin),
            Just(Event::SetXToMax),
            Just(Event::SetYToMin),
            Just(Event::SetYToMax),
            any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),
            Just(Event::Blur),
        ]
    }

    proptest! {
        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]

        #[test]
        #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
        fn area_event_sequences_keep_value_valid(
            events in prop::collection::vec(arb_event(), 0..64),
        ) {
            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(
                Props { id: "ca".into(), ..Props::default() },
                &Env::default(),
                &Default::default(),
            );

            for ev in events {
                drop(svc.send(ev));
            }

            let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});

            let value = *api.value();

            prop_assert!((0.0..360.0).contains(&value.hue));
            prop_assert!((0.0..=1.0).contains(&value.saturation));
            prop_assert!((0.0..=1.0).contains(&value.lightness));

        #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
        fn picker_focus_index_stays_in_bounds(
            count in 1usize..8,
            columns in 1usize..5,
            events in prop::collection::vec(arb_event(), 0..64),
        ) {
            let colors: Vec<ColorValue> = (0..count)
                .map(|index| ColorValue::from_hsl((index as f64) * 30.0, 1.0, 0.5))
                .collect();

            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(
                Props { id: "csp".into(), colors, columns, ..Props::default() },
                &Env::default(),
                &Default::default(),
            );

            for ev in events {
                drop(svc.send(ev));
            }

            let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});

            if let Some(idx) = api.focused_index() {
                prop_assert!(idx < count, "focused index {idx} >= {count}");
            }

            // The listbox root's rendered `data-ars-state` is always one of the
            // two declared states.
            let root = api.root_attrs();

            prop_assert_eq!(root.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("listbox"));

            let state = root
                .get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"))
                .expect("root exposes data-ars-state");

            prop_assert!(state == "idle" || state == "focused");
        }
    }
}

qr_pixel_size_matches_formula_and_dimensions(\n            props in arb_props(),\n            matrix in arb_opt_matrix(),\n        ) {\n            let api = Api::new(&props, matrix, &Env::default(), &Messages::default());\n\n            let expected = match api.matrix() {\n                Some(matrix) => {\n                    (matrix.size + props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * props.module_size\n                }\n                None => 0.0,\n            };\n\n            prop_assert!((api.pixel_size() - expected).abs() < f64::EPSILON);\n\n            let attrs = api.root_attrs();\n            let ## Correction: proptests added

You caught a genuine miss in my Phase 3 coverage audit. I had claimed "stateless peers carry no proptests," but `separator` (also stateless) **does** — it proptests its prop→attr branch invariants over the randomized input space. QrCode has exactly analogous branches, so it warrants the same. Per the no-deferral rule, I landed it in this PR rather than deferring.

**Added** to [crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs](crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs) — a `qr_code_proptests` module (7 properties, `#[ignore]`d for the nightly `extended-proptest` job, same as the color machines):

| Property | Pins |
|---|---|
| `qr_part_dispatch_equals_attrs` | `part_attrs(p)` ≡ each `*_attrs()` for all 5 parts |
| `qr_root_always_has_scope_part_role` | scope=`qr-code`, part=`root`, role=`img` always present |
| `qr_label_follows_url_branch` | link vs plain aria-label across the http(s) branch |
| `qr_id_emitted_iff_non_empty` | `id` attr present ⟺ `props.id` non-empty |
| `qr_pixel_size_matches_formula_and_dimensions` | quiet-zone formula (and `0.0` with no matrix) + rendered `{n}px` styles |
| `qr_overlay_src_emitted_iff_present` | `src` attr present ⟺ `overlay_src` is `Some` |
| `qr_download_trigger_is_labelled_button` | always `type=button` + the download label |

The `arb_value()` strategy deliberately mixes plain text and http/https URLs so the link-label branch is actually exercised; `arb_matrix()` builds square `n×n` grids so `size == n` holds.

**Verified:** all 7 pass (1000 cases each); `cargo clippy -p ars-components --tests --all-features` clean.

Revised test breadth for the component: **12 unit + 8 snapshot + 7 proptest + 1 doctest + 1 spec-conformance**.

May I now run `cargo xci-fast` and then commit + open the PR (`Closes #288`)?

{
  "command": "cd /Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-288\ngh pr create --base main --head impl-288 \\\n  --title \"Implement QrCode agnostic core (#288)\" \\\n  --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nImplements the framework-agnostic stateless core of `QrCode` in `ars-components::specialized` (issue #288, epic #227). A declarative component that maps an input value to a rendered QR matrix with an accessible label, error-correction level, sizing, colors, an optional center overlay, and an optional download trigger.\n\n## Design decision: matrix is adapter-injected\n\nThe agnostic core defines only the **rendering contract** (`QrMatrix`, `Api`, `Part`). `Api::new(props, matrix, env, messages)` takes the matrix as an injected `Option<QrMatrix>` supplied by the caller. This:\n\n- keeps the core dependency-free and `no_std`-compatible (no QR-encoding crate added),\n- matches both adapter specs, which state *\"the adapter owns encoding even though the core spec defines rendering semantics.\"*\n\nThe core spec's `Api::new` previously called an **undefined `QrMatrix::generate`** and a **non-existent `generate_id`**. Both are corrected in this PR (spec-sync): generation is the adapter's job, and the root `id` is emitted only when non-empty (the established convention, mirroring `color_swatch`). The Leptos/Dioxus adapter sketches are updated to the new signature.\n\n## Anatomy\n\n`Root` (role=img, sized, URL-aware aria-label), `Frame`, `Pattern`, `Overlay` (src when set), `DownloadTrigger` (type=button + label).\n\n## Tests\n\n- **12 unit** + **8 snapshot** (every part + each output-affecting branch: URL vs plain label, id present/absent, matrix Some/None, overlay src present/absent)\n- **7 proptest** (nightly `extended-proptest`) pinning the prop→attr mapping across the input space, mirroring `separator`'s stateless proptests\n- **1 doctest** documenting the matrix-injection contract\n- **1 spec-conformance** anatomy test\n\n`qr_code/mod.rs` has full line coverage. `cargo xci-fast` passes all 10 gates.\n\n## Post-implementation audit\n\nRan the mandatory 3-phase audit. Findings landed in this PR: a `# Examples` doctest (Phase 2), the `Overlay`-without-`src` snapshot and the full proptest suite (Phase 3 — the proptest gap was surfaced during review).\n\nCloses #288\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Create the PR targeting main"
}

> AGENT

Assessment of the four Codex findings (Codex is correct on all four; verified against spec + crate conventions):

- **T1 (P3, URL case-insensitivity):** Correct — `starts_with("https://")` misses `HTTPS://`; schemes are case-insensitive. Clear fix via `eq_ignore_ascii_case` on the scheme prefix. **Will implement.**
- **T3 (P2, overlay `alt=""`):** Correct — the Overlay `<img>` needs a decorative `alt=""` (the QR's aria-label already conveys content). `HtmlAttr::Alt` exists. **Will implement.**
- **T4 (P2, sanitize `module_size`):** Correct and matches crate convention (`meter`/`angle_slider` both guard `is_finite()` / `<= 0.0`). Non-finite/non-positive `module_size` would emit `NaNpx`/`-44px`. **Will implement** (fall back to the `4.0` default, mirroring `angle_slider`).
- **T2 (P2, `role="img"` hides interactive `DownloadTrigger`):** Technically correct — `role="img"` is a leaf role, so a `DownloadTrigger` button rendered under Root is pruned from the a11y tree. This is a real flaw in the **spec's** anatomy (§2 nests DownloadTrigger under a role=img Root; §3.1 says Root=img). Fixing it reshapes the documented accessibility contract, so I want your call on direction.

> TOOL

  "new_string": "    /// The module size used for rendering, normalized to a positive, finite\n    /// value.\n    ///\n    /// A non-finite or non-positive `module_size` prop (`0.0`, negative, `NaN`,\n    /// or infinity) would otherwise produce invalid `width`/`height` styles such\n    /// as `-44px` or `NaNpx`, so it falls back to the default module size.\n    fn effective_module_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.props.module_size.is_finite() && self.props.module_size > 0.0 {\n            self.props.module_size\n        } else {\n            DEFAULT_MODULE_SIZE\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// The accessible name for the QR image, using the link-specific template\n    /// when the value is an http(s) URL.\n    fn aria_label(&self) -> String {\n        if is_url(&self.props.value) {\n            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        } else {\n            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including the quiet zone).\n    ///\n    /// Returns `0.0` when no matrix has been supplied. The module size is\n    /// normalized via [`Api::effective_module_size`] so non-finite or\n    /// non-positive props never reach the rendered dimensions.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        match &self.matrix {\n            Some(matrix) => {\n                (matrix.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.effective_module_size()\n            }\n\n            None => 0.0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the root element.\n    ///\n    /// The root is a neutral sized container. from the accessibility tree by a `role=\"img\"` ancestor.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);\n\n        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());\n        }\n\n        let size = self.pixel_size();\n\n        attrs\n            .set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!(\"{size}px\"))\n            .set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!(\"{size}px\"));\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the optional decorative frame.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn frame_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        part_attrs(&Part::Frame)\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the QR module grid.\n    ///\n    /// Carries `role=\"img\"` and the URL-aware `aria-label`: the pattern element\n    /// is the QR code's accessible image.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pattern_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = part_attrs(&Part::Pattern);\n\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"img\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.aria_label());\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for the optional centered overlay image.\n    ///\n    /// The overlay is decorative: the QR pattern already exposes the encoded\n    /// content via its `aria-label`, so the `<img>` is marked presentational\n    /// with an empty `alt`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn overlay_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = part_attrs(&Part::Overlay);\n\n        if let Some(src) = &self.props.overlay_src {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Src, src.clone()).set(HtmlAttr::Alt, \"\");\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }"

  "new_string": "/// Returns the localized `aria-label` for the download trigger.\ntype DownloadLabelFn = dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync;\n\n/// Default pixel size of a single QR module, used by [`Props::default`] and as\n/// the fallback when a caller supplies a non-finite or non-positive\n/// `module_size`.\nconst DEFAULT_MODULE_SIZE: f64 = 4.0;\n\n/// Whether `value` is an http(s) URL, comparing the scheme case-insensitively\n/// (URL schemes are case-insensitive per RFC 3986).\nfn is_url(value: &str) -> bool {\n    value\n        .get(..7)\n        .is_some_and(|prefix| prefix.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"http://\"))\n        || value\n            .get(..8)\n            .is_some_and(|prefix| prefix.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"https://\"))\n}"
}

let connected = api(&props, Some(sample_matrix()));\n\n        // The pattern (SVG) carries the image semantics...\n        let pattern = connected.pattern_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(pattern.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"img\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            pattern.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"QR code: hello\")\n        );\n\n        // ...and the root does NOT, so an interactive DownloadTrigger rendered\n        // inside the root is not pruned from the accessibility tree.\n        let root = connected.root_attrs();\n        assert!(!root.contains(&HtmlAttr::Role));\n        assert!(!root.contains(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn url_value_uses_link_label() {\n        let https = Props {\n            value: \"https://example.com\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n\n        assert_eq!(\n            api(&https, Some(sample_matrix()))\n                .pattern_attrs()\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"QR code linking to https://example.com\")\n        );\n\n        let http = Props {\n            value: \"http://example.com\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n\n        assert_eq!(\n            api(&http, Some(sample_matrix()))\n                .pattern_attrs()\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"QR code linking to http://example.com\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn url_scheme_is_matched_case_insensitively() {\n        for value in [\"HTTPS://example.com\", \"Http://example.com\", \"HtTpS://x.io\"] {\n            let props = Props {\n                value: value.to_string(),\n                ..Props::default()\n            };\n\n            assert_eq!(\n                api(&props, Some(sample_matrix()))\n                    .pattern_attrs()\n                    .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n                Some(alloc::format!(\"QR code linking to {value}\").as_str()),\n                \"scheme of {value:?} should be recognized as a URL\"\n            );\n        }\n\n        // A value that merely contains \"http\" later is not a URL.\n        let not_url = Props {\n            value: \"see http://x later\".to_string(),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        assert_eq!(\n            api(&not_url, Some(sample_matrix()))\n                .pattern_attrs()\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"QR code: see http://x later\")\n        );\n    }"

  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn overlay_attrs_sets_src_and_decorative_alt_only_when_present() {\n        let without = Props::default();\n        let bare = api(&without, None).overlay_attrs();\n\n        assert!(!bare.contains(&HtmlAttr::Src));\n        assert!(!bare.contains(&HtmlAttr::Alt));\n\n        let with = Props {\n            overlay_src: Some(\"/logo.png\".to_string()),\n            ..Props::default()\n        };\n        let decorated = api(&with, None).overlay_attrs();\n\n        assert_eq!(decorated.get(&HtmlAttr::Src), Some(\"/logo.png\"));\n        // Decorative overlay: empty alt so AT does not announce the file name.\n        assert_eq!(decorated.get(&HtmlAttr::Alt), Some(\"\"));\n    }"
}

380	            "[a-zA-Z0-9 ]{0,24}",
381	            r"https://[a-z]{1,12}\.[a-z]{2,4}",
382	            r"http://[a-z]{1,12}\.[a-z]{2,4}",
383	        ]
384	    }

388	            id in "[a-z]{0,8}",
389	            value in arb_value(),
390	            error_correction in arb_error_correction(),
391	            module_size in 1.0f64..20.0,
392	            quiet_zone in 0usize..10,
393	            overlay_src in proptest::option::of("[a-z./]{1,16}"),
394	            overlay_size in 0.0f64..0.5,

403	                overlay_size,
404	                ..Props::default()
405	            }

409	    prop_compose! {
410	        /// A square matrix of `n` rows of `n` modules, so `size == n`.
411	        fn arb_matrix()(n in 1usize..10)(
412	            rows in prop::collection::vec(
413	                prop::collection::vec(any::<bool>(), n..=n),
414	                n..=n,
415	            ),
416	        ) -> QrMatrix {
417	            QrMatrix::new(rows)
418	        }
419	    }

422	        proptest::option::of(arb_matrix())

431	        fn qr_part_dispatch_equals_attrs(
432	            props in arb_props(),

  "old_string": "    /// A mix of plain text and URL values so the link-label branch is exercised.\n    fn arb_value() -> impl Strategy<Value = String> {\n        prop_oneof![\n            \"[a-zA-Z0-9 ]{0,24}\",\n            r\"https://[a-z]{1,12}\\.[a-z]{2,4}\",\n            r\"http://[a-z]{1,12}\\.[a-z]{2,4}\",\n        ]\n    }\n\n    prop_compose! {\n        fn arb_props()(\n            id in \"[a-z]{0,8}\",\n            value in arb_value(),\n            error_correction in arb_error_correction(),\n            module_size in 1.0f64..20.0,\n            quiet_zone in 0usize..10,\n            overlay_src in proptest::option::of(\"[a-z./]{1,16}\"),\n            overlay_size in 0.0f64..0.5,\n        ) -> Props {\n            Props {\n                id,\n                value,\n                error_correction,\n                module_size,\n                quiet_zone,\n                overlay_src,\n                overlay_size,\n                ..Props::default()\n            }\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// A mix of plain text and URL values (including mixed-case schemes) so the\n    /// link-label branch and its case-insensitive scheme match are exercised.\n    fn arb_value() -> impl Strategy<Value = String> {\n        prop_oneof![\n            \"[a-zA-Z0-9 ]{0,24}\",\n            r\"https://[a-z]{1,12}\\.[a-z]{2,4}\",\n            r\"http://[a-z]{1,12}\\.[a-z]{2,4}\",\n            r\"(?i:https)://[a-z]{1,12}\\.[a-z]{2,4}\",\n            r\"(?i:http)://[a-z]{1,12}\\.[a-z]{2,4}\",\n        ]\n    }\n\n    /// Module sizes spanning both the valid range and the invalid values\n    /// (`0.0`, negative, `NaN`, infinities) that must fall back to the default.\n    fn arb_module_size() -> impl Strategy<Value = f64> {\n        prop_oneof![\n            1.0f64..20.0,\n            Just(0.0),\n            -20.0f64..0.0,\n            Just(f64::NAN),\n            Just(f64::INFINITY),\n            Just(f64::NEG_INFINITY),\n        ]\n    }\n\n    /// Mirrors `Api::effective_module_size`: a non-finite or non-positive\n    /// `module_size` falls back to the default `4.0`.\n    fn effective_module_size(module_size: f64) -> f64 {\n        if module_size.is_finite() && module_size > 0.0 {\n            module_size\n        } else {\n            4.0\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Mirrors `qr_code::is_url`: an http(s) scheme matched case-insensitively.\n    fn is_url(value: &str) -> bool {\n        value\n            .get(..7)\n            .is_some_and(|prefix| prefix.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"http://\"))\n            || value\n                .get(..8)\n                .is_some_and(|prefix| prefix.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"https://\"))\n    }\n\n    prop_compose! {\n        fn arb_props()(\n            id in \"[a-z]{0,8}\",\n            value in arb_value(),\n            error_correction in arb_error_correction(),\n            module_size in arb_module_size(),\n            quiet_zone in 0usize..10,\n            overlay_src in proptest::option::of(\"[a-z./]{1,16}\"),\n            overlay_size in 0.0f64..0.5,\n        ) -> Props {\n            Props {\n                id,\n                value,\n                error_correction,\n                module_size,\n                quiet_zone,\n                overlay_src,\n                overlay_size,\n                ..Props::default()\n            }\n        }\n    }"
}

140	    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including quiet zone).
141	    /// Returns `0.0` when no matrix has been supplied.
142	    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {
143	        match &self.matrix {
144	            Some(matrix) => (matrix.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.props.module_size,
145	            None => 0.0,
146	        }
147	    }
148	
149	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
150	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
151	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
152	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
153	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
154	        // The id is adapter-supplied; emit it only when set (no in-core id generation).
155	        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {
156	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());
157	        }
158	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "img");
159	        let label = if self.props.value.starts_with("http://") || self.props.value.starts_with("https://") {
160	            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)
161	        } else {
162	            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)
163	        };
164	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), label);
165	        let size = self.pixel_size();
166	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!("{size}px"));
167	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!("{size}px"));
168	        attrs
169	    }
170	
171	    pub fn frame_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
172	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
173	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Frame.data_attrs();
174	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
175	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
176	        attrs
177	    }
178	
179	    pub fn pattern_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
180	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
181	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Pattern.data_attrs();
182	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
183	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
184	        attrs
185	    }
186	
187	    pub fn overlay_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
188	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
189	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Overlay.data_attrs();
190	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
191	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
192	        if let Some(src) = &self.props.overlay_src {

198	    pub fn download_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {

201	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
202	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);

205	        attrs
206	    }

211	
212	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
213	        match part {
214	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
215	            Part::Frame => self.frame_attrs(),

217	            Part::Overlay => self.overlay_attrs(),

223	
224	## 2. Anatomy
225	
226	```text
227	QrCode
228	├── Root             (required — role="img", container for the QR code)
229	├── Frame            (optional — decorative frame around the code)
230	├── Pattern          (required — the actual QR module grid, rendered as SVG or Canvas)
231	├── Overlay          (optional — centered image/logo)
232	└── DownloadTrigger  (optional — button to download QR code as image)
233	```
234	
235	| Part            | Element    | Key Attributes                          |
236	| --------------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------- |
237	| Root            | `<div>`    | `role="img"`, `aria-label`, sized to QR |
238	| Frame           | `<div>`    | decorative border                       |
239	| Pattern         | `<svg>`    | QR module grid rendering                |
240	| Overlay         | `<img>`    | `src` from `overlay_src` prop           |
241	| DownloadTrigger | `<button>` | `aria-label` from messages              |
242	
243	## 3. Accessibility
244	
245	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
246	
247	| Part | Role  | Properties                                                     |
248	| ---- | ----- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
249	| Root | `img` | `aria-label` — `"QR code: {value}"` (or link variant for URLs) |
```

emit it only when set (no in-core id generation).\n        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());\n        }\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"img\");\n        let label = if self.props.value.starts_with(\"http://\") || self.props.value.starts_with(\"https://\") {\n            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        } else {\n            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), label);\n        let size = self.pixel_size();\n        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!(\"{size}px\"));\n        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!(\"{size}px\"));\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    pub fn frame_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Frame.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    pub fn pattern_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Pattern.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    pub fn overlay_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Overlay.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        if let Some(src) = &self.props.overlay_src {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Src, src.clone());\n        }\n        attrs\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// The module size used for rendering, normalized to a positive, finite\n    /// value. A non-finite or non-positive `module_size` prop falls back to the\n    /// default so `width`/`height` can never become `-44px` or `NaNpx`.\n    fn effective_module_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.props.module_size.is_finite() && self.props.module_size > 0.0 {\n            self.props.module_size\n        } else {\n            DEFAULT_MODULE_SIZE\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// The accessible name for the QR image, using the link-specific template\n    /// when the value is an http(s) URL.\n    fn aria_label(&self) -> String {\n        if is_url(&self.props.value) {\n            (self.messages.link_label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        } else {\n            (self.messages.label)(&self.props.value, &self.locale)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including quiet zone).\n    /// Returns `0.0` when no matrix has been supplied.\n    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        match &self.matrix {\n            Some(matrix) => (matrix.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.effective_module_size(),\n            None => 0.0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// The root is a neutral sized container. The image semantics live on\n    /// `pattern_attrs` so the optional interactive `DownloadTrigger` is not\n    /// pruned from the accessibility tree by a `role=\"img\"` ancestor.\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        // The id is adapter-supplied; emit it only when set (no in-core id generation).\n        if !self.props.id.is_empty() {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.props.id.clone());\n        }\n        let size = self.pixel_size();\n        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Width, format!(\"{size}px\"));\n        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Height, format!(\"{size}px\"));\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    pub fn frame_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Frame.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// The pattern (SVG) is the QR code's accessible image, carrying `role=\"img\"`\n    /// and the URL-aware `aria-label`.\n    pub fn pattern_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Pattern.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"img\");\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.aria_label());\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    pub fn overlay_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Overlay.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        // Decorative overlay: the pattern already exposes the content via its\n        // aria-label, so the <img> is marked presentational with an empty alt.\n        if let Some(src) = &self.props.overlay_src {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Src, src.clone());\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Alt, \"\");\n        }\n        attrs\n    }"
}

Accessibility\n\n### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties\n\n| Part | Role  | Properties                                                     |\n| ---- | ----- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| Root | `img` | `aria-label` — `\"QR code: {value}\"` (or link variant for URLs) |\n\nThe QR code is a purely visual element.   "new_string": "QrCode\n├── Root             (required — neutral sized container for the QR code)\n├── Frame            (optional — decorative frame around the code)\n├── Pattern          (required — the QR module grid, role=\"img\", rendered as SVG or Canvas)\n├── Overlay          (optional — centered decorative image/logo)\n└── DownloadTrigger  (optional — button to download QR code as image)\n```\n\n| Part            | Element    | Key Attributes                              |\n| --------------- | ---------- | ------------------------------------------- |\n| Root            | `<div>`    | sized to QR (no role — neutral container)   |\n| Frame           | `<div>`    | decorative border                           |\n| Pattern         | `<svg>`    | `role=\"img\"`, `aria-label`, QR module grid  |\n| Overlay         | `<img>`    | `src` from `overlay_src` prop, `alt=\"\"`     |\n| DownloadTrigger | `<button>` | `aria-label` from messages                  |\n\n## 3. Accessibility\n\n### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties\n\n| Part    | Role  | Properties                                                     |\n| ------- | ----- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| Pattern | `img` | `aria-label` — `\"QR code: {value}\"` (or link variant for URLs) |\n\nThe QR code is a purely visual element. The accessible image semantics live on\nthe `Pattern` (the SVG that visually is the code), not on `Root`: `role=\"img\"`\nis a leaf role that prunes its descendants from the accessibility tree, so\nputting it on `Root` would hide the interactive `DownloadTrigger`. The\n`aria-label` conveys the encoded data; if the data is a URL (scheme matched\ncase-insensitively), the label indicates that (e.g., `\"QR code linking to {url}\"`).\nThe `Overlay` image is decorative (`alt=\"\"`) since the `Pattern` already conveys\nthe content."

=== LEPTOS 40-58 ===
## 4. Part Mapping

| Core part / structure | Required? | Adapter rendering target | Ownership     | Attr source                    | Notes                                        |
| --------------------- | --------- | ------------------------ | ------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------- |
| `Root`                | required  | `<div>`                  | adapter-owned | `api.root_attrs()`             | Carries `role="img"` and resolved label.     |
| `Frame`               | optional  | `<div>`                  | adapter-owned | `api.frame_attrs()`            | Decorative wrapper when themed.              |
| `Pattern`             | required  | `<svg>`                  | adapter-owned | `api.pattern_attrs()`          | Canonical render target for SSR-safe output. |
| `Overlay`             | optional  | `<img>`                  | adapter-owned | `api.overlay_attrs()`          | Render only when `overlay_src` is present.   |
| `DownloadTrigger`     | optional  | `<button>`               | adapter-owned | `api.download_trigger_attrs()` | Render only when download is enabled.        |

## 5. Attr Merge and Ownership Rules

| Target node       | Core attrs                     | Adapter-owned attrs                   | Consumer attrs    | Merge order                   | Ownership notes                               |
| ----------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| `Root`            | role, label, size, scope, part | none beyond wrapper class merge       | decoration attrs  | root semantics and sizing win | consumer styling must not remove `role="img"` |
| `Pattern`         | QR matrix rendering attrs      | generated `<path>` or module children | none beyond style | generated pattern wins        | rendering stays adapter-owned                 |
| `DownloadTrigger` | button attrs and label         | click handler                         | decoration only   | label and handler win         | download trigger remains optional             |

## 6. Composition / Context Contract
=== LEPTOS 190-195 ===

## 26. Adapter Invariants

- The root always remains `role="img"` with a resolved label.
- The QR pattern remains renderable during SSR.
- Download support never blocks baseline rendering.

> AGENT

Addressed all 4 Codex findings (TDD, no coverage regression). `xci-fast` is verifying before push. Summary of what I changed:

- **T1:** `is_url()` now matches the scheme case-insensitively (`HTTPS://` recognized).
- **T2:** `role="img"` + `aria-label` moved from `root_attrs` to `pattern_attrs` (Root is now a neutral sized container, so a `DownloadTrigger` inside it stays in the a11y tree). Spec §1.2/§2/§3.1 + both adapter specs synced.
- **T3:** `overlay_attrs` now emits decorative `alt=""` alongside `src`.
- **T4:** `module_size` is sanitized (`effective_module_size` falls back to `4.0` for non-finite/non-positive), so dimensions can't become `-44px`/`NaNpx`.

Tests added/updated (TDD), snapshots regenerated, proptests updated, 0 uncovered lines. Once `xci-fast` passes I'll push, reply to + resolve each thread, and re-trigger `@codex review`.

264	    /// A non-finite or non-positive `module_size` prop (`0.0`, negative, `NaN`,

  "old_string": "    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including the quiet zone).\n    ///\n    /// Returns `0.0` when no matrix has been supplied. The module size is\n    /// normalized via [`Api::effective_module_size`] so non-finite or\n    /// non-positive props never reach the rendered dimensions.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        if let Some(matrix) = &self.matrix {\n            (matrix.size + self.props.quiet_zone * 2) as f64 * self.effective_module_size()\n        } else {\n            0.0\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Total pixel size of the rendered QR code (including the quiet zone).\n    ///\n    /// Returns `0.0` when no matrix has been supplied. The module size is\n    /// normalized via [`Api::effective_module_size`] so non-finite or\n    /// non-positive props never reach the rendered dimensions. The module\n    /// count is summed in `f64` so a large `quiet_zone` cannot overflow the\n    /// `usize` arithmetic (which would panic in debug or wrap in release).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pixel_size(&self) -> f64 {\n        if let Some(matrix) = &self.matrix {\n            let modules_per_side = matrix.size as f64 + self.props.quiet_zone as f64 * 2.0;\n            modules_per_side * self.effective_module_size()\n        } else {\n            0.0\n        }\n    }"
}

=== leptos 136-141 ===
## 19. Consumer Expectations and Guarantees

- Consumers may assume the QR output is available without client-only APIs.
- Consumers may assume the root is labeled as an image.
- Consumers must not assume download support exists on every runtime.

=== leptos 196-220 ===

## 27. Accessibility and SSR Notes

Root labeling must remain present even when the encoded value is a URL or when an overlay is shown. Decorative frame and overlay content must not replace the root label.

## 28. Parity Summary and Intentional Deviations

Parity summary: full core parity.

Intentional deviations: the adapter standardizes on SVG as the primary rendering target for hydration-safe output.

## 29. Test Scenarios

- baseline SVG QR rendering
- URL-specific accessible label
- overlay present and absent
- download trigger hidden or disabled when export support is unavailable

## 30. Test Oracle Notes

| Behavior         | Preferred oracle type | Notes                                                    |
| ---------------- | --------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| root semantics   | DOM attrs             | assert role and accessible label                         |
| QR structure     | rendered structure    | assert SVG root and matrix-derived children or path data |
| download cleanup | cleanup side effects  | assert object URLs are revoked                           |
```

> AGENT

Round-2 Codex findings addressed:
- **T5:** completed the spec sync — leptos & dioxus consumer-guarantee, accessibility-notes, and test-oracle sections now describe **pattern**-owned labeling (root is a neutral container). No more internal contradiction.
- **T6:** `pixel_size` now sums module counts in `f64`, so a large `quiet_zone` can't overflow the `usize` math. Added `large_quiet_zone_does_not_overflow_sizing` and broadened the proptest's `quiet_zone` strategy (incl. `usize::MAX`).

`xci-fast` is verifying. Once green I'll push, reply to + resolve both threads, and re-trigger `@codex review`.
